Method: 1) 对每个正则参考轨迹做精确插入构造，借助局部化向量势移除集中奇异包周围的背景，使非线性交叉项消失，修正后的力在奇异时间仍保持光滑；<br>2) 给出支撑集构造、导数估计、分数阶Sobolev缩放和经典lifespan论证；<br>3) 证明插入轨迹在能量与耗散范数下强收敛；<br>4) 对s≥1/2，采用临界力bootstrap，结合H^1估计与高Sobolev延拓得到正则开集，从而证明非稠密性。

Result: 稠密性结果：光滑力在继承的时间积分空间H^s拓扑中稠密，当且仅当s<1/2。<br>非稠密性结果：在s=1/2及以上，正则开集存在，因而稠密性失效。<br>附加结果涉及扩展数据投影、每个固定光滑初始速度切片、混合力范数、无穷维变分以及内部no-slip实现。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了奇异驱动力集合在H^s拓扑中的稠密性阈值恰为s=1/2，并通过局部化向量势插入给出正结果，通过临界bootstrap与正则性延拓给出负结果。构造方法改变的是驱动力，而非对单一给定力分类奇异初始速度。

Abstract: Starting from the compact, smoothly forced Navier--Stokes blowup solution constructed by OpenAI, we study the distribution of the singular data it generates. For fixed viscosity and time horizon on the three-dimensional torus, smooth forces producing classical breakdown from rest by that time are dense in the inherited time-integrated spatial $H^s$ topology if and only if $s<1/2$ (the norms are defined in Section~\ref{sec:intro}). The positive result follows from an exact insertion into every regular reference trajectory. A localized vector potential removes the background around a concentrated singular packet, so all nonlinear cross terms vanish and the modified force remains smooth through the singular time. We give the full support construction, derivative estimates, fractional Sobolev scaling and classical-lifespan argument. The inserted trajectories converge strongly in the energy and dissipation norm. A separate critical-force bootstrap, followed by an $H^1$ estimate and high-Sobolev continuation, supplies the regular open set needed for non-density at and above $s=1/2$. Further results describe extended-data projections, every fixed smooth initial-velocity slice, mixed force norms, infinite-dimensional variations and interior no-slip realizations. The construction varies the force; it does not classify singular initial velocities for a single prescribed force.

</details>

Abstract: We address the plasma-vacuum interface problem in the two dimensional space. The plasma region is governed by the ideal incompressible magnetohydrodynamic equations. The vacuum region is described by the pre-Maxwell dynamics, with the unknowns set to zero for simplicity. This is a free boundary problem of ideal Euler type equations. Under the strong horizontal magnetic field in the plasma region, we prove the global in time nonlinear stability of the two dimensional plasma-vacuum interface. The proof relies on understanding the interplay between the dynamics of the fluids inside the domain and those on the free interface, as well as the inherent structures of the problem.

</details>

Method: 以一个抽象框架为核心工具，刻画超压缩性、非局部衰减估计与一致L^p界之间的关系，并据此证明广义Stokes算子的L^p映射性质。

Conclusion: 该抽象框架有效统一了超压缩性、非局部衰减与L^p有界性，从而系统性地建立了广义Stokes算子的sharp L^p映射理论。

Abstract: In this article, we establish $\mathrm{L}^p$-mapping properties for the generalized Stokes operator with bounded measurable coefficients on the full range of exponents that is known to be sharp for elliptic systems with rough coefficients. These include $\mathrm{L}^p$-estimates for the corresponding semigroup, its gradient and the $\mathrm{H}^\infty$-calculus, as well as $\mathrm{L}^q$-$\mathrm{L}^p$ smoothing estimates. As a key ingredient, we develop an abstract framework that captures the relationship between hypercontractivity, non-local decay estimates and uniform $Ł^p$-bounds.

</details>


### [88] [Finite-time divergence of kinetic-energy fluctuations in a Schrödinger-induction flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10085)
*Weishuo Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文在约束 Schrödinger–induction 系统中构造了一个由流体奇异性驱动的动力学能量涨落模型：概率密度与平均动能保持有界，但规范不变的动能方差以 $(1-t)^{-1/2-5h}$ 速率发散；并刻画了端点态仅属于形式域而非算子域（第一动能谱矩有限、第二谱矩无穷）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察流体奇异性（流体集中）如何影响量子/波动表述中的动能涨落，评估水动力学波动表述下平均能量控制方法的适用边界，并建立流体集中与动能域正则性丧失之间的联系。

Method: 方法上采用约束 Schrödinger–induction 系统，引用零初速度的 Navier–Stokes 构造及其精确外部剖面，由常值归一化波函数与零联络出发构造周期轨道；外力在归一化奇异时刻 $t=1$ 具有光滑时空延拓；通过对完整速度模的四次方在振荡修正消失的精确外部区域上积分，得到发散下界；并在常值波函数规范下考察弱 $L^2$ 联络极限及其磁形式给出的正自伴动力学算子。

Result: 结果为：概率密度保持常数、平均物质动能保持有界，而规范不变动能方差发散，下界增长阶为 $(1-t)^{-1/2-5h}$（$h$ 由源构造固定）；外部区域外存在唯一的弱 $L^2$ 联络极限但不属于 $L^4$；归一化端点态属于算子形式域而不属于算子域，第一动能谱矩有限、第二动能谱矩为无穷。

Conclusion: 结论是流体集中会导致动能域正则性的丧失，该构造为评估水动力学波动表述中平均能量控制的极限提供了一个动力学基准。

Abstract: In this study, we use a constrained Schrödinger--induction system to investigate kinetic-energy fluctuations induced by a fluid singularity. Assuming the cited Navier--Stokes construction with zero initial velocity and its exact exterior profile, we construct a periodic trajectory from a constant normalized wave function and zero connection. The prescribed body force admits a smooth space--time extension across the normalized singular time $t=1$. In this construction, the probability density remains constant and the mean matter kinetic energy stays bounded, whereas the gauge-invariant kinetic-energy variance diverges. We prove a lower growth bound of order $(1-t)^{-1/2-5h}$, with $h$ fixed by the source construction, by integrating the fourth power of the complete velocity's magnitude over an exact exterior region where all oscillatory corrections vanish. After fixing a gauge with constant wave function, we identify a unique weak $L^2$ connection limit outside $L^4$ and construct its positive self-adjoint kinetic operator through the associated magnetic form. We further show that the normalized endpoint state belongs to the form domain but not the operator domain: its first kinetic spectral moment is finite, whereas its second is infinite. These results connect fluid concentration to a loss of kinetic-domain regularity and provide a dynamical benchmark for assessing the limits of mean-energy control in hydrodynamic wave formulations.

</details>


### [89] [Secular Instability and the Turning-Point Principle for Rigidly Rotating Viscous Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10098)
*Ming Cheng,Zhiwu Lin,Yucong Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究刚性旋转黏性星的轴对称稳定性。作者证明线性化NSP生成元的失稳指标（按Riesz代数重数计）等于固定质量与总角动量下增广能量的Morse指标；建立了一个适用于有限负刚度指标的阻尼陀螺方程的抽象Kelvin–Tait–Chetaev定理（无需紧性假设、无需零处谱隙，刚度核可为无穷维）；对NSP应用，因黏性耗散退化，先投影掉由刚性平移与刚性转动生成的轴对称核，再用守恒律与Routh约化把失稳指标传回完整生成元。结果表明：固定总角动量下慢旋转分支的黏性失稳始于首个非退化球面质量极大值的延拓；而对任意足够小的非零角动量，相应的Euler–Poisson星在该极大值之后的一段区间内仍保持轴对称谱稳定。因此耗散可在无黏星失稳之前使旋转星失稳，其阈值的分离源于黏性对角动量的重新分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性旋转星的稳定性通常用无黏Euler–Poisson模型判断，但真实星体存在黏性耗散，而耗散是否会改变失稳阈值并不清楚。核心问题是：黏性耗散（尤其当其在轴对称子空间上退化时）如何影响旋转星的轴对称稳定性阈值，以及能否用变分/Morse指标方法在缺乏紧性和谱隙的情形下严格刻画失稳指标。

Method: 对象为自由边界Navier–Stokes–Poisson系统描述的刚性旋转黏性星。首先证明线性化NSP生成元的失稳指标（含Riesz代数重数）等于固定质量与总角动量下增广能量的Morse指标。为此建立有限负刚度指标的阻尼陀螺方程抽象Kelvin–Tait–Chetaev定理，不要求紧性或零处谱隙，允许无穷维刚度核。应用时处理黏性耗散的退化性：先投影掉由刚性轴向平移和刚性转动张成的轴对称核，再借助守恒律与Routh约化，将抽象定理得到的失稳指标转回完整NSP生成元。

Result: 在固定总角动量下，慢旋转分支的黏性失稳起始于首个非退化球面质量极大值的延拓。相反，对任意足够小的非零角动量，相应的Euler–Poisson星在该极大值之后的一段区间内仍保持轴对称谱稳定。由此耗散可在对应的无黏星失稳之前使旋转星失稳。阈值分离的原因是黏性对角动量的重新分布：它取消了无黏情形对物质角动量分布的约束，但保持总角动量不变。

Conclusion: 黏性耗散可能提前触发旋转星的轴对称失稳，使黏性稳定性阈值与无黏阈值发生分离，机制是角动量的黏性再分布。所建立的抽象Kelvin–Tait–Chetaev定理与Morse指标等式为退化耗散、无谱隙、非紧情形下的失稳计数提供了通用工具。

Abstract: We study axisymmetric stability of rigidly rotating viscous stars modeled by the free-boundary Navier--Stokes--Poisson (NSP) system. The unstable index of the linearized NSP generator, counted with Riesz algebraic multiplicity, equals the Morse index of the augmented energy at fixed mass and total angular momentum. We prove an abstract Kelvin--Tait--Chetaev theorem for damped gyroscopic equations with finite negative stiffness index, requiring no compactness assumptions and no spectral gap at zero; the stiffness kernel may be infinite-dimensional. In the NSP application, the viscous dissipation is degenerate: its axisymmetric kernel, generated by rigid axial translation and rigid rotation, is projected out before applying the abstract theorem, while conservation laws and a Routh reduction transfer the resulting instability index back to the full NSP generator. For slowly rotating branches with fixed total angular momentum, viscous instability begins at the continuation of a first nondegenerate spherical mass maximum. By contrast, for every sufficiently small nonzero angular momentum, the corresponding Euler--Poisson star remains axisymmetrically spectrally stable on an interval beyond this maximum. Thus dissipation can destabilize a rotating star before the corresponding inviscid star becomes unstable. This separation of the stability thresholds results from viscous redistribution of angular momentum, which removes the inviscid constraints on its material distribution while preserving its total value.

</details>


### [90] [Controlled harmonic map heat flow and blow-up from the disk to the sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10129)
*Shengquan Xiang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究圆盘到球面的一旋转调和映射热流在Dirichlet边界控制下的爆破与全局存在性。引入控制适配的粘合策略构造显式上剖面与良制备边界迹：低于某上剖面且边界值大于π的非负初值在常值迹下爆破，而在对应的良制备控制下全局存在；时间导数一致有界的控制可防止无穷时间集中。边界控制可驱动任意非负初值在任意指定时间前爆破，但有界控制无法防止足够快的集中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射热流的爆破现象是几何分析中的核心问题。本文旨在通过边界控制这一外部手段，理解并调控爆破行为，区分哪些控制能阻止爆破、哪些无法阻止，从而刻画爆破与可控性之间的关系。

Method: 采用控制适配的粘合策略（control-adapted gluing strategy）以防止爆破。构造显式的上剖面（upper profiles）与良制备边界迹（well-prepared boundary traces），将初值、边界值与所用控制类型进行匹配分析。

Result: 1）低于某上剖面且边界值大于π的非负初值，在对应常值迹下爆破；2）在相应的良制备边界迹以下的任意控制下，解全局存在；3）时间导数一致有界的控制可防止无穷时间集中；4）边界控制能驱动任意非负初值在任意预设时间前爆破；5）有界控制无法阻止足够快的集中。

Conclusion: 边界控制是调控一旋转调和映射热流爆破的有效工具：良制备控制可实现全局存在，而选取不当的控制仍导致爆破；有界控制存在本质局限，无法防止快速集中，但可加速爆破。

Abstract: We study the one-corotational harmonic map heat flow from the disk to the sphere with a Dirichlet boundary control. We introduce a control-adapted gluing strategy to prevent blow-up. More precisely, we construct explicit upper profiles and well-prepared boundary traces such that every nonnegative datum below one of these profiles and having boundary value above $π$ blows up under the corresponding constant trace, whereas the corresponding solution is global under every control below the associated well-prepared boundary trace. Controls with uniformly bounded time derivative also prevent infinite-time concentration. We also show that boundary control can drive any nonnegative datum to blow-up before any prescribed time, whereas bounded controls cannot prevent suitably fast concentration.

</details>


### [91] [Bramson correction and convergence to the critical wave for Fisher-KPP equations on $\mathbb Z^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10143)
*Yuanyang Hu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathbb Z^d$ ($d\ge2$) 上带最近邻扩散的 Fisher-KPP 方程，初始数据非零且具紧支集，证明沿各带符号坐标轴前沿的对数延迟、过渡区域的均匀有界宽度，以及半管区域内解收敛到最小速度格点行波的平移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Fisher-KPP 前沿传播理论多集中于连续介质或一维情形；在高维格点上，理解沿各方向前沿的精确位置（尤其延迟量与过渡区结构）以及解如何局部趋于行波，是反应扩散方程传播理论的核心问题。本文旨在给出高维格点上非平面传播的定量刻画，并不假设反应项的凹性，以推广既有结果。

Method: 证明采用加权估计、倾斜随机游走的界，以及乘积下解的构造；通过沿各带符号坐标轴建立对数延迟，并利用这些工具控制过渡区域宽度与相位有界性。

Result: 证明沿每条带符号坐标轴存在对数延迟；过渡区域具有均匀有界宽度；在每个绕轴固定宽度的半管上，解收敛到最小速度格点行波的平移，且相位有界；同时得到每个方向上带对数修正的上界。

Conclusion: 结果在高维格点上精确刻画了 Fisher-KPP 前沿的延迟与局部行波行为，且不依赖反应项凹性假设，推广了先前相关结论。

Abstract: We consider Fisher-KPP equations with nearest-neighbor diffusion on $\mathbb Z^d$, $d\geq2$, with nonzero finitely supported initial data. We prove a logarithmic delay of the front along each signed coordinate axis and show that the transition region has uniformly bounded width. On every fixed-width half-tube around an axis, the solution converges to translates of the minimal-speed lattice traveling wave, with a bounded phase. We also obtain an upper bound with a logarithmic correction in every direction. The proof uses weighted estimates, bounds for tilted random walks, and a product lower solution. Concavity of the reaction is not assumed.

</details>


### [92] [The shallow--water limit of linear water waves over a discontinuous bottom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10170)
*Martin Oen Paulsen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文严格证明了带陡坎底部（sharp bottom step）上线性水波的零色散极限，将浅水方程恢复为一个透射问题，并刻画了不连续处产生的局部色散效应与边界层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带陡坎的底部地形上，浅水参数趋于零时水波极限行为尚缺乏严格数学论证，尤其是陡坎处速度势会形成角点奇异性、产生局部色散效应，经典浅水方程无法描述这一局部现象。论文旨在建立均匀估计框架并刻画这种由陡坎诱发的色散边界层。

Method: 1. 在二维Lipschitz曲面底部的流体域上建立抽象泛函框架，基于Dirichlet–Neumann算子的分数次幂；2. 用适配的Rellich恒等式刻画低正则能量空间，获得对浅水参数一致估计；3. 对陡坎几何使用显式共形映射，得到Grisvard平移定理的一个版本并给出势函数奇异部分的显式公式；4. 通过水平变量依赖性分析确定陡坎产生的窄过渡尺度；5. 数值验证并识别陡坎诱导的色散边界层，进而在强色散区域严格推导描述该边界层的色散模型。

Result: 建立了对浅水参数一致的估计框架；得到了势函数奇异部分显式公式，揭示陡坎生成的窄过渡尺度；证明在远离过渡区时解收敛到浅水透射问题，而在陡坎附近保留局部色散修正；数值验证了该差异，并识别出陡坎诱导的色散边界层；对小陡坎严格导出了显式描述该边界层的色散模型。

Conclusion: 带陡坎底部的线性水波零色散极限由浅水透射问题描述，但陡坎处残留局部色散效应，形成过渡区内的色散边界层；通过显式奇异分析、数值验证与强色散区域建模，完整刻画了该边界层结构。

Abstract: We rigorously justify the zero--dispersion limit of linear water waves over a sharp bottom step, recovering the shallow-water equations as a transmission problem, and describe the localized dispersive effects generated near the discontinuity. To obtain estimates uniform in the shallow-water parameter, we study an abstract functional framework for the linear water waves equations in two-dimensional fluid domains with a Lipschitz curved bottom. The framework is based on fractional powers of the Dirichlet--Neumann operator, and we characterize low-regularity energy spaces using adapted Rellich identities. At higher regularity, the associated velocity potential develops corner singularities.
  For the step geometry, we use an explicit conformal mapping to obtain a version of Grisvard's shift theorem, where we derive an explicit formula for the singular part of the potential. The dependence on the horizontal variable reveals a narrow transition scale generated by the step. The convergence toward the shallow-water transmission problem holds away from this transition region, while a localized dispersive correction remains near the step. We illustrate this discrepancy numerically and identify the resulting step-induced dispersive boundary layer. Building on these observations, we consider the strongly dispersive regime and rigorously derive a dispersive model that explicitly describes this boundary layer for a small step.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部完全非线性方程（如分数阶2-Hessian型方程）的适定性与正则性理论尚不完善，已有文献通常需要较强的凸性假设且局限于部分s范围。作者希望刻画决定算子行为的系数矩阵类及其退化结构，并在更弱假设、全s范围下建立一致椭圆性与正则性，同时使结果在s→1时与经典局部理论相容。

Method: 研究对象为inf_{A∈A₂} Δ^s(u∘A)(A^{-1}x)形式的非局部2-Hessian方程。首先刻画系数矩阵集合A₂，给出其可能退化情形的几何描述；进而在此几何框架下分析算子的一致椭圆性。所用几何方法为本文新引入，即使在局部情形亦具新意，并适用于更广泛的非局部完全非线性方程与曲率型问题。

Result: 主要定理表明：当右端项严格为正时，非局部2-Hessian方程保持一致椭圆性，由此得到正则性估计；结果在弱于既有文献的假设下成立，覆盖s∈(0,1)全范围，且不要求解具有凸性；假设条件可解释为半凹性与2-凸性的非局部对应物；所有结果在s→1时稳定并恢复局部情形。

Conclusion: 该工作通过刻画A₂的几何与退化结构，在弱假设、全分数阶范围及无需凸性的条件下建立了非局部2-Hessian方程的一致椭圆性与正则性理论，并保证局部极限的稳定性；所发展的几何方法具有独立价值，可推广至更一般的非局部完全非线性方程与曲率型问题。

Abstract: We study a nonlocal 2-Hessian equation, given by an infimum of linear deformations of the fractional Laplacian, that is, $\inf_{A\in {A}_2}Δ^s (u\circ A)(A^{-1}x)$. We characterize the class of coefficient matrices ${A}_2$, which determines the behavior of the operator, and provide a detailed geometric description of the possible degeneracies. Our main theorem shows that the nonlocal 2-Hessian equation remains uniformly elliptic for strictly positive right-hand sides, which leads to regularity estimates. The results hold under weaker hypotheses than those previously considered in the literature, and for the full range $s\in(0,1)$. In particular, no convexity on the solutions is required. Our hypotheses can be interpreted as nonlocal counterparts of the local notions of semiconcavity and 2-convexity. Moreover, all the results are stable as $s\to1$, recovering the local case. The geometric methods developed here are new, even in the local setting, and may be relevant to a broader class of nonlocal fully nonlinear equations and curvature-type problems.

</details>


### [94] [Identifying unknown time delay and spatially varying coefficients in a reaction-diffusion equation from boundary measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10222)
*Ming-Hui Ding,Hongyu Liu,Catharine W. K. Lo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 首次在带空间变系数的时滞抛物型PDE中，仅用边界通量测量同时辨识未知时滞τ与空间异质系数：利用初始历史与边界数据不相容产生的奇异时间行为反演τ，再用Carleman不等式建立Lipschitz稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往时滞方程反问题通常假设时滞τ已知，且局限于ODE或抽象框架；而实际多时滞反应扩散系统中τ与空间变系数均未知，亟需在抛物型PDE框架下实现二者联合反演。

Method: 研究对象为含多重时滞（时滞扩散、时滞时间导数、时滞源项）的一般线性反应扩散系统，仅以边界通量测量为数据。首先利用给定初始历史与边界数据不相容所诱发的奇异时间行为来辨识时滞τ；在τ确定后，针对时间无关系数p=p(x)、q=q(x)，借助Carleman不等式建立Lipschitz稳定性估计。

Result: 成功同时反演出未知时滞τ与空间异质系数；在时间无关系数情形下获得Lipschitz稳定性估计，且方法适用于具空间变系数的抛物型PDE，而非仅ODE或抽象设定。

Conclusion: 这是首次在带时滞的抛物型PDE中，仅凭边界数据同时恢复未知时滞与空间依赖系数；其核心创新在于利用不兼容性产生的奇异时间行为分离并确定τ，为多时滞反应扩散系统的系数反演提供了可行途径。

Abstract: This work investigates an inverse problem for a general class of linear reaction-diffusion systems incorporating multiple delayed contributions, namely retarded diffusion, retarded time derivatives, and retarded source terms. The objective is to simultaneously recover the unknown time lag $τ>0$ and spatially heterogeneous coefficients from boundary flux measurements alone. The recovery strategy exploits the singular temporal behavior generated by an incompatibility between the prescribed initial history and the boundary data. In contrast to prior inverse problems for delay equations, which assume $τ$ known and are confined to ODE or abstract settings, our approach operates in a parabolic PDE framework with spatially varying coefficients. Once $τ$ is identified, we establish a Lipschitz stability estimate via a Carleman inequality, in the case of time-independent coefficients $p=p(x)$, $q=q(x)$. This is the first result to simultaneously recover an unknown delay and spatially dependent coefficients in a delayed parabolic PDE from boundary data.

</details>


### [95] [Convergence Rate toward Rarefaction Wave under Periodic Perturbation for Generalized Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10223)
*Lin Chang,Yuling Xu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究广义Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers方程在空间周期扰动下的稀疏波稳定性，证明当初始扰动适当小时，方程解趋于稀疏波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期扰动在远场持续振荡，导致解与稀疏波之间存在持续的振荡差异，使得常规稳定性分析方法难以直接应用，因此该稳定性问题具有挑战性和重要性。

Method: 构造一个与解具有相同振荡结构的合适拟设（ansatz），通过解与拟设之间的相消使得剩余扰动落入适当的Sobolev空间，再利用能量方法证明非线性稳定性。

Result: 若初始扰动围绕稀疏波适当小，则广义Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers方程的解趋于稀疏波，即稀疏波在空间周期扰动下具有稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了周期扰动下稀疏波的稳定性理论，其核心思想是通过匹配振荡结构的拟设实现相消并借助能量方法完成证明，为处理远场持续振荡的稳定性问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: In this paper, a rarefaction wave under space-periodic perturbation for the generalized Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers equation is considered. It is shown that if the initial perturbation around the rarefaction wave is suitably small, then the solution of the system tends to the rarefaction wave. The stability of solution under periodic perturbation is an interesting and important problem since the perturbation keeps oscillating at the far fields.
  The key of proof is to construct a suitable ansatz carrying the same oscillation as the solution. Then we can find cancellations between solutions and ansatz such that the perturbation belongs to some Sobolev space. The nonlinear stability can be obtained by the energy method.

</details>


### [96] [Quantitative analysis of ground states for the fractional logarithmic Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10254)
*Xiaoming An,Shuangjie Peng,Fulin Zhong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了分数阶对数Schrödinger方程 $(-\Delta)^s Q=Q\log Q$ 在 $\mathbb{R}^N$ 中的正基态在平移意义下唯一且非退化，并应用于建立尖锐的分数阶对数Sobolev不等式及其等号情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分数阶对数Schrödinger方程正基态的唯一性与非退化性。主要困难在于势 $-1-\log Q$ 在 $\mathbb{R}^N$ 上无界，导致无法直接使用现有的有界势分数阶Schrödinger算子的径向振荡理论。

Method: 对有界势进行逼近以克服势无界的困难；利用相关二次型Morse指标为一以及角分解得到非退化性；基于对数基态的孤立性和分数幂方程的唯一性理论，通过次临界幂非线性变分逼近证明唯一性。

Result: 对任意 $N\geq1$ 和 $0<s<1$，正基态在平移意义下唯一且非退化，即线性化算子 $L_Q=(-\Delta)^s-1-\log Q$ 的核为 $\operatorname{span}\{\partial_{x_1}Q,\cdots,\partial_{x_N}Q\}$。

Conclusion: 作为应用，建立了尖锐的分数阶对数Sobolev不等式并刻画了所有等号成立的情形。

Abstract: Let $N\geq1$ and $0<s<1$. We study positive ground states of the fractional logarithmic Schrödinger equation \begin{equation*} (-Δ)^sQ=Q\log Q \quad\text{in }\mathbb{R}^N. \end{equation*} We prove that for every $N\geq1$ and $0<s<1$, the positive ground state is unique up to translations and nondegenerate. More precisely, for the linearized operator $L_Q=(-Δ)^s-1-\log Q$, it holds that \begin{equation*} \ker L_Q=\operatorname{span}\{\partial_{x_1}Q,\cdots,\partial_{x_N}Q\}. \end{equation*} A main difficulty is that the potential $-1-\log Q$ is unbounded in $\mathbb{R}^N$, which prevents a direct application of the available radial oscillation theory for fractional Schrödinger operators with bounded potentials. We overcome this difficulty by a bounded-potential approximation. Using also the fact that the associated quadratic form has Morse index one and an angular decomposition, we obtain the nondegeneracy. Based on the isolation of logarithmic ground states and the uniqueness theory for the fractional power equation, we prove uniqueness by a variational approximation with subcritical power nonlinearities. As an application, we establish sharp fractional logarithmic Sobolev inequalities and characterize all cases of equality.

</details>


### [97] [Singular Forces on the Whole Space: Sobolev Density Thresholds and Energy Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10269)
*Shaozhen Cao,Zhuoni Chi*

Main category: math.AP

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望将已有紧支、光滑外力驱动的 Navier–Stokes 爆破构造推广到全空间情形，并在相对时空拓扑中精确确定外力集合的稠密/非稠密阈值，同时控制低频空间频率、避免依赖周期均值或 Poincaré 不等式。

Method: 从既有紧支光滑外力驱动的爆破构造出发，先对正则背景做紧支无散度移除，再精确插入单个全空间奇异波包；配合分离的临界估计证明非稠密性，并显式控制低频部分。

Result: 对每个固定正粘性与截止时间，导致经典爆破的光滑外力在相对 $L^1_tH^s_x$ 中稠密当且仅当 $s<1/2$，在相对 $L^2_tH^s_x$ 中稠密当且仅当 $s<-1/2$；正结果保持任意固定 $H^\infty_\sigma(\R^3)$ 初速度。另获能量–外力完备空间中的稠密性、正则轨道的强能量–耗散逼近，以及给定有限族全空间网格上单元观测的精确相等。

Conclusion: 结论适用于时空紧支外力及 Clay 问题中快速衰减数据类；文中区分初态空间、外力空间与数值误差正则性；不声称固定外力不稳定性、弱存在性丧失或新的形式化验证。

Abstract: We derive whole-space distribution results from the established compact, smoothly forced Navier--Stokes blowup construction of OpenAI. For every fixed positive viscosity and deadline, smooth forces causing classical breakdown from rest on $\mathbb R^3$ are dense in the relative $L^1_tH^s_x$ topology exactly when $s<1/2$, and in the relative $L^2_tH^s_x$ topology exactly when $s<-1/2$. The positive results preserve every fixed initial velocity in $H^\infty_σ(\R^3)$. They follow from a compact divergence-free removal of a regular background, followed by exact insertion of a single whole-space singular packet. Separate critical estimates prove non-density; low spatial frequencies are controlled explicitly, without a periodic mean or a Poincaré inequality. The conclusions hold for spacetime-compact forces and for the rapidly decaying data class in the Clay formulation. We also obtain density in the usual completed energy-force spaces, strong energy-and-dissipation approximation of regular trajectories, and exact equality of cell observations on a prescribed finite family of whole-space grids. Initial-state spaces, forcing spaces and numerical error regularity are distinguished throughout. No claim of fixed-force instability, loss of weak existence or new formal verification is made.

</details>


### [98] [The Schrödinger equation with fluctuating nonlinearity in the energy space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10417)
*Max Sauerbrey,Joris van Winden*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究了带非线性Stratonovich噪声的非线性Schrödinger方程，结合随机Strichartz估计与经典方法，得到了局部适定性、爆破替代及全局适定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进已有随机非线性Schrödinger方程结果中的非线性指数范围p、q以及噪声的空间正则性假设。

Method: 在能量空间H^1(R^d;C)中，将[Potential Anal. 41 (2014)]的随机Strichartz估计与[Ann. Inst. H. Poincaré Phys. Théor. 46 (1987)]的方法相结合，处理非线性Stratonovich噪声项。

Result: 对所有能量次临界非线性p,q∈[1,1+4/(d-2)_+)，得到局部适定性及相应爆破替代；对线性乘性噪声q=1、实值噪声W和散焦非线性λ≤0，通过能量有界性验证爆破条件，得到全局适定性；当p,q均为质量次临界∈[1,1+4/d)时，给出涉及L^2范数的改进爆破判据，并利用实值W的质量守恒得到全局适定性。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了非线性指数范围并放宽了噪声空间正则性假设，确立了相应条件下的局部与全局适定性。

Abstract: We study nonlinear Schrödinger equations with nonlinear Stratonovich noise
  \begin{equation*}
  \mathrm{d} u\,=\, i\bigl[ Δu \,+\, λ|u|^{p-1}u\bigr] \, \mathrm{d} t \,+\,i|u|^{(q-1)/2}u\circ \mathrm{d} {W},
  \end{equation*} in their energy space $H^1(\mathbb R^d;\mathbb C)$. By combining the stochastic Strichartz estimates derived in [Potential Anal. 41 (2014), pp.\ 269--315] with the approach from [Ann.\ Inst.\ H.\ Poincaré Phys.\ Théor.\ 46 (1987), pp.\ 113--129] we obtain local well-posedness for all energy-subcritical nonlinearities $p,q\in [1, 1+4/(d-2)_+)$ together with a corresponding blow-up alternative. For a linear multiplicative noise $q=1$, a real-valued noise $W$ and a defocusing nonlinearity $λ\le 0$, we check this blow-up condition using a bound on the energy, resulting in the global well-posedness of the equation. If both nonlinearities are mass-subcritical, i.e., $p,q\in [1, 1+4/d)$, we provide an improved blow-up criterion involving the $L^2(\mathbb R^d;\mathbb C)$-norm. Using the conservation of mass for real-valued $W$, we obtain global well-posedness also in this case. Compared to previous results on stochastic nonlinear Schrödinger equations, we thereby improve the range of exponents $p$ and $q$ and the spatial regularity assumption on the noise.

</details>


### [99] [Open Inextensible Filaments in Planar Stokes Flow: Well-Posedness, Endpoint Asymptotics, and Straightening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10480)
*Han Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究平面Stokes流体中自由端不可伸长开曲线的适定性、端点展开与全局动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自由端不可伸长细丝在平面Stokes流体中的数学结构，特别是低正则初值下的适定性、端点奇性行为及长期收敛性。

Method: 将系统约化为三阶非局部曲率方程与张力椭圆方程耦合；在满足弧弦条件的Sobolev空间$\widetilde H^s$($-1/2<s\le0$)证明局部适定性；用Wiener--Hopf因子分解推导自由端$d^{3/2}$型展开；通过能量恒等式和弯曲能与弧弦常数的几何估计证明全局存在与指数收敛。

Result: 对近临界初值证明局部适定性，对正时间证明正则性提升及每个自由端的$d^{3/2}$展开；对充分小初值及满足$E(0)<\pi^2/4$的有限能量初值证明全局存在与指数收敛到直线；有限时间破裂必伴随弧弦条件失效；全局解或指数收敛于直线，或弧弦常数沿趋于无穷的时间序列趋于零。

Conclusion: 建立了自由端Stokes细丝的完整数学理论框架，揭示端点$d^{3/2}$奇性结构与能量阈值$\pi^2/4$决定的全局行为二分性。

Abstract: We study an inextensible open filament with free ends in a planar Stokes fluid. The system reduces to a third-order nonlocal curvature equation coupled to an elliptic equation for the tension. We prove local well-posedness for nearly critical initial data in supported Sobolev spaces $\widetilde H^s$, $-1/2<s\le0$, satisfying the arc-chord condition. For positive times, we prove improved Sobolev regularity and derive a $d^{3/2}$-type expansion at each free end using Wiener--Hopf factorization, where $d$ denotes the distance to that endpoint. We further prove global existence and exponential convergence to a straight filament for sufficiently small initial data and for finite-energy initial data satisfying $E(0)<π^2/4$. The finite-energy result follows from an energy identity and a geometric estimate relating the bending energy to the arc-chord constant. More generally, any finite-time breakdown must be accompanied by loss of the arc-chord condition, while every global solution either converges exponentially to a straight filament or has arc-chord constants tending to zero along a sequence of times tending to infinity.

</details>

### [100] [Spacetime closedness theorem for homogeneous Lorentzian foliations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09181)
*Robert Monjo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在洛伦兹层面表述并证明了一个"时空封闭性定理"：从大爆炸与时间控制这一纯洛伦兹假设出发，导出切片体积有限，再借助齐性与完备性把有限体积提升为紧致性；定理适用于任意维度，并给出 Lean 4 形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的 space-form 分类定理只能刻画齐性、各向同性的空间几何，其本身并不能给出关于外围时空的洛伦兹陈述；已有结果多依赖黎曼化或额外结构，缺少直接在洛伦兹层面陈述的封闭性定理，因此需要在洛伦兹设置下重新建立相应结论。

Method: 在极大正规的全局双曲 (n+1) 维时空上设定有限时刻大爆炸与齐性完备类空切片，从真正洛伦兹性的分层控制假设（或较弱版本：以累积膨胀率的时间可积性替代极大正规性）出发，先做分析步骤——由大爆炸与时间控制推出切片体积有限，再做几何步骤——由齐性与完备性把有限体积升级为紧致性。该论证对任意时空维度成立，并对强、弱两种抽象陈述进行 Lean 4 形式化。

Result: 在强版本（极大正规、全局双曲、有限时刻大爆炸、齐性完备类空切片）与弱版本（以累积膨胀率的时间可积性替代极大正规性）下均得到切片体积有限，并进一步得到时空的封闭性/紧致性结论；定理在任意维度下成立，且强、弱抽象陈述均已通过 Lean 4 形式化验证。

Conclusion: 该工作把空间几何的经典分类思想提升为洛伦兹层面的时空封闭性定理，证明可清晰分为分析步骤与几何步骤，且不依赖黎曼化结构；结果具有任意维一般性与机器验证支撑。

Abstract: Motivated by the geometric interpretation of spatially homogeneous cosmological models, we formulate a spacetime closedness theorem directly at the Lorentzian level. A classical space-form classification theorem organizes homogeneous and isotropic spatial geometries, but by itself it does not yield a Lorentzian statement about the ambient spacetime. The main technical step is to derive finite slice-volume from genuinely Lorentzian control hypotheses on the foliation. This is achieved in a strong version, for maximally regular globally hyperbolic $(n+1)$--spacetimes with a finite-time Big Bang and homogeneous complete spacelike slices, and in a weaker version in which maximal regularity is replaced by time-integrability of the accumulated expansion rate. In both cases, the argument separates an analytic step, deriving finite slice-volume from the Big Bang and temporal control, from a geometric step, upgrading finite volume to compactness by homogeneity and completeness. The theorem is stated in arbitrary spacetime dimension and is accompanied by a Lean~4 formalization of the strong and weak abstract statements.

</details>


### [101] [Area-constrained Willmore surfaces in Lorentzian manifolds: curvature inequalities and the Hawking quasi-local energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09416)
*Alejandro Peñuela Diaz*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文建立了四维洛伦兹流形中闭类空曲面的面积约束Willmore理论，引入沿指定法方向的"方向临界性"，并在连接相容方向上证明了尖锐不等式 2λ|Σ|+∫_Σ|H⃗|²dμ ≤ 8πχ(Σ)，在主导能量条件与 λ≥0 下推出 ∫_Σ|H⃗|²dμ ≤ 16π，且等号情形具刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在洛伦兹几何中研究面积约束Willmore泛函时，欧拉—拉格朗日方程取值于法丛而非单个函数，导致经典的临界性概念无法直接使用；同时Hawking拟局域能量的正性、刚性、驻定性与无穷小单调性等性质需要统一的变分框架来刻画，因此需要引入新的"方向临界性"概念。

Method: 引入沿指定法方向的"方向临界性"，并借助法联络定义一类"连接相容方向"；在Einstein张量符号条件下，对连接相容的类空方向进行变分分析，证明尖锐不等式；进一步在主导能量条件与λ≥0下导出能量上界；通过超曲面表述将Hawking曲面问题还原为空间法方向上的临界性问题；并在球对称、Kerr与FLRW时空中构造显式例子。

Result: 证明了尖锐不等式 2λ|Σ|+∫_Σ|H⃗|²dμ ≤ 8πχ(Σ)（连接相容类空方向，Einstein张量符号条件下）；在主导能量条件与λ≥0时得到 ∫_Σ|H⃗|²dμ ≤ 16π。等号成立时具刚性：Σ为圆球型并满足Minkowski因果菱形刚性；对零临界方向也证明了类似的尖锐不等式。研究给出了Hawking拟局域能量的正性、刚性、驻定性与无穷小单调性陈述；超曲面表述恢复了既有Hawking曲面问题。

Conclusion: 该工作以方向临界性为核心，为洛伦兹面积约束Willmore问题提供了统一变分理论，并给出Hawking拟局域能量的多项结构性质；球对称、Kerr与FLRW的显式例子表明方向临界性与整体临界性确有区别，也说明刚性理论中符号假设的必要性。

Abstract: We develop an area-constrained Willmore theory for closed spacelike surfaces in four-dimensional Lorentzian manifolds. Since the Euler--Lagrange equation is normal-bundle-valued, we introduce directional criticality along a prescribed normal direction and identify a class of connection-compatible directions determined by the normal connection. Our main result is the sharp inequality $2λ|Σ|+\int_Σ|\vec H|^2,dμ\leq 8πχ(Σ)$ for criticality in a connection-compatible spacelike direction under an Einstein-tensor sign condition. Under the dominant energy condition and $λ\geq0$, this yields $\int_Σ|\vec H|^2,dμ\leq16π$. Equality is rigid: $Σ$ is round and satisfies Minkowski causal-diamond rigidity. We also prove an analogous sharp inequality for null critical directions.
  These results give positivity, rigidity, stationarity, and infinitesimal monotonicity statements for the Hawking quasi-local energy. A hypersurface formulation recovers the previously studied Hawking-surface problem as criticality in the spatial normal direction. Explicit examples in spherical symmetry, Kerr, and FLRW spacetimes distinguish directional from full criticality and show the necessity of the sign assumptions in the rigidity theory.

</details>


### [102] [Conformal boundary rigidity for simple Finsler metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09527)
*Kelvin Lam,Hanming Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明简单Finsler流形是共形稳定的：对给定简单Finsler流形及一类共形因子，刻画其诱导边界距离函数的奇性，从而定义合适的$H^2$范数，并在该共形因子类上得到关于此Sobolev范数和$L^2$范数的稳定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Finsler流形上的共形稳定性问题，将经典的边界距离稳定性结果推广到Finsler情形。

Method: 将Mukhometov的经典分部积分技术适配到Finsler框架；通过刻画共形因子诱导边界距离函数的奇性来定义合适的$H^2$范数。

Result: 证明了简单Finsler流形具有共形稳定性，并建立了关于所定义$H^2$ Sobolev范数与$L^2$范数的稳定性估计。

Conclusion: 简单Finsler流形是共形稳定的，Mukhometov的分部积分方法可有效推广至Finsler设定。

Abstract: In this paper we prove that simple Finsler manifolds are conformally stable. Given a simple Finsler manifold and a class of conformal factors, we characterize the singularity of their induced boundary distance functions which allows us to define an appropiate $H^2$ norm. We then obtain a stability estimate with respect to this Sobolev norm and the $L^2$ norm on the class of the conformal factors. To prove the main theorems, we adapt the classical integration by parts technique employed by Mukhometov \cite{Muhometov} to the Finsler setting.

</details>


### [103] [Obata-type equations under the Bakry-Émery Ricci curvature conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09712)
*Mijia Lai,Yiwei Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用由带Robin边界条件的Obata型方程所决定的warped product结构，在适当的Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率条件及其他假设下，建立了带光滑边界的紧致流形上的若干刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带边界紧致流形在Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率条件下的刚性分类问题，借助warped product结构与Obata型方程刻画达到刚性时的几何形态，推广经典Obata刚性结论至带Robin边界的加权情形。

Method: 基于带Robin边界条件的Obata型方程所诱导的warped product结构，结合Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率下界条件及附加假设，对紧致带边流形进行刚性分析。

Result: 在Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率条件与相应假设下，得到带光滑边界紧致流形的一系列刚性结果（满足等号情形时流形必为特定的warped product结构）。

Conclusion: warped product结构是刻画该类带Robin边界Obata型方程刚性现象的有效框架，所得结果将经典刚性定理拓展到加权曲率与带边界设定。

Abstract: In this paper, based on the warped product structures determined by the Obata-type equations with Robin boundary condition, we establish some rigidity results for compact manifolds with smooth boundary under appropriate Bakry-Émery Ricci curvature conditions and some other assumptions.

</details>


### [104] [Product structures and decompositions of $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09750)
*Jin Matsui*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立 $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-对称空间的分解与乘积理论，实现非平凡型半单黎曼情形下的局部分类，并揭示两类特有现象：存在不同乘积结构，以及存在无穷小各向同性表示为平凡的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典对称空间（$\Gamma\cong\mathbb{Z}_2$）已有成熟的分解、乘积与分类理论，而 $\Gamma\cong\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ 的推广情形缺乏相应的结构理论。作者希望补全这一理论空白，并考察推广后是否出现经典情形不具备的新现象。

Method: 以 $\Gamma\cong\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ 的 $\Gamma$-对称空间为对象，建立其分解定理与乘积理论；在此基础上对非平凡型的半单黎曼 $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-对称空间进行局部分类；并通过构造性分析辨识其特有现象。

Result: 建立了 $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-对称空间的分解与乘积理论；完成了非平凡型半单黎曼 $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-对称空间的局部分类；并发现两例独有现象——存在不同的乘积结构，以及存在无穷小各向同性表示为平凡的实例。

Conclusion: $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-对称空间的理论框架被系统建立并完成相应局部分类，且其结构与表示层面均出现区别于经典对称空间的本质新特征，表明该推广并非平凡延伸。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the decomposition and product theory of $Γ$-symmetric spaces, where $Γ$ is isomorphic to $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$. These results lead to the local classification of semisimple Riemannian $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric spaces of nontrivial type. Furthermore, we identify notable phenomena specific to $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric spaces: the existence of distinct product structures and that of an example with the trivial infinitesimal isotropy representation.

</details>


### [105] [Multiply warped products with harmonic Weyl tensor and zero radial Weyl curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09759)
*M. A. R. M. Horácio*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究一维基上多重warped积在调和Weyl张量与零径向Weyl曲率条件下的局部几何，证明纤维数至多为三，并刻画其度量结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画同时满足调和Weyl张量与零径向Weyl曲率这类兼具共形与曲率约束的流形结构，明确其纤维数量的上界与局部几何分类。

Method: 采用共形变换将warping函数化为二次多项式，并结合Ricci张量给出的相容方程进行推导。

Result: 证明该类流形最多含三个纤维并给出局部几何描述；所得度量或由局部共形平坦多重warped积将空间形式纤维替换为Einstein流形得到，或属于纤维维数相等、Einstein常数为正的三纤维族；例子表明纤维数上界是sharp的，且两个Weyl条件互不蕴含。

Conclusion: 给出了此类多重warped积的完整局部几何分类，并讨论了soliton型方程带来的更强限制。

Abstract: In this article, we study multiply warped products with a one-dimensional base, harmonic Weyl tensor and zero radial Weyl curvature. We prove that these manifolds have at most three fibers and describe their local geometry. The proof uses a conformal change which makes the warping functions quadratic polynomials, together with the compatibility equations imposed by the Ricci tensor. The resulting metrics are either obtained from locally conformally flat multiply warped products by replacing their space-form fibers with Einstein manifolds, or belong to a three-fiber family with equal fiber dimensions and positive Einstein constants. Examples show that the bound is sharp and that neither Weyl condition implies the other. We also discuss the stronger restrictions arising from soliton-type equations.

</details>


### [106] [On the Asymptotics of the Volume of Hitchin Moduli Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09795)
*Shiyu Cao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了固定奇次行列式的秩二无迹Higgs丛模空间$\M$在Hitchin度量下测地球的体积渐近公式，并给出系数$2^{4g-3}\pi^{9g-9}/(3g-3)!$，同时确定了Hamiltonian次水平体积与指数加权体积的领头渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hitchin模空间作为超凯勒流形，其度量几何与体积增长是几何拓扑与规范理论交叉的核心问题；本文旨在精确给出测地球体积的领头渐近及其系数，并推广到次水平体积与加权体积。

Method: 证明结合了超凯勒体积形式的齐次性与正则Hitchin轨迹上的度量渐近；通过辛约化与Prym极化计算系数，并用等变局部化独立验证。

Result: 对每个$p\in\M$，$\Vol_{g_{L^2}}B_{L^2}(p,R)=\frac{2^{4g-3}\pi^{9g-9}}{(3g-3)!}R^{6g-6}+o(R^{6g-6})$；同时确定了Hamiltonian次水平体积与指数加权体积的领头渐近，系数可被独立复现。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了Hitchin模空间测地球体积的精确增长速度与系数，揭示了体积增长由$6g-6$次幂主导，并提供了两种一致的计算路径。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a compact Riemann surface of genus $g\geq2$, and let $\M$ be the moduli space of rank-two trace-free Higgs bundles with fixed determinant of odd degree. For the normalization of the Hitchin metric used in this paper, we prove that the volume of geodesic ball in the Hitchin moduli space is given by $$ \Vol_{g_{L^2}}B_{L^2}(p,R) =\frac{2^{4g-3}π^{9g-9}}{(3g-3)!}\,R^{6g-6}+o(R^{6g-6}) $$ for every $p\in\M$. We also determine the leading asymptotics of Hamiltonian sublevel volumes and exponentially weighted volumes. The proof combines homogeneity of the hyperkähler volume form with metric asymptotics on the regular Hitchin locus. Symplectic reduction and the Prym polarization evaluate the coefficient, which is independently recovered by equivariant localization.

</details>


### [107] [Outward minimizing $p$-capacity, horizons, and Schwarzschild rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09813)
*Sehong Park*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在非负标量曲率、渐近平坦的单端流形$(\R^3\setminus\{0\})$上，证明了ADM质量$m_+$被Schwarzschild归一化$p$-容量$c_{O,p}$从下方控制，并刻画了等号情形与黑洞视界的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正质量定理的定量加强形式：寻找能下界ADM质量且能探测视界（horizon，即最小面积/ outward-minimizing 边界）的几何量。容量型不等式有望统一质量下界与黑洞存在性判据。

Method: 考虑具有两个端（一端渐近平坦、ADM质量$m_+$）的完备黎曼流形$(M^3,g)$，标量曲率非负。对$p\in(1,3)$，在分离两端的outward-minimizing有限周长边界上取Schwarzschild归一化$p$-容量的下确界，定义$c_{O,p}$；同时引入无约束容量下确界$c_{M,p}$及分离两端边界面积下确界$A(g)$。通过变分/极小曲面与层析（capacity）比较论证质量不等式及等号刚性。

Result: 在$A(g)>0$时证得$m_+\ge c_{O,p}$。单个指数处取等即推出存在最小面积视界（least-area horizon），其外部必为质量$m_+$的Schwarzschild外部，并且在所有指数处均取等。等号情形下，若第二端也渐近平坦，则$m_-\ge m_+$，且等号当且仅当流形为双侧空间Schwarzschild流形。此外，$c_{O,p}$与$c_{M,p}$之间存在严格间隙可探测视界的存在。

Conclusion: 容量型不等式$m_+\ge c_{O,p}$给出了ADM质量的定量下界，并蕴含强刚性：取等迫使Schwarzschild几何与视界出现。约束容量与无约束容量的间隙提供了视界存在的判据，推广了质量-容量方法在黑洞探测中的应用。

Abstract: Let $(M^3,g)$ be a complete Riemannian manifold diffeomorphic to $\R^3\setminus\{0\}$, with nonnegative scalar curvature. Assume that a distinguished end is asymptotically flat, with ADM mass $m_+$. For each $p\in(1,3)$, define $c_{O,p}$ as the infimum of the Schwarzschild-normalized $p$-capacity over outward-minimizing finite-perimeter boundaries separating the two ends. We prove that $m_+\ge c_{O,p}$ whenever $A(g)>0$, where $A(g)$ is the infimum of the areas of boundaries separating the two ends. Equality at a single exponent produces a least-area horizon, forces its exterior to be the Schwarzschild exterior of mass $m_+$, and yields equality at every exponent. In the equality case, if the second end is also asymptotically flat, its mass satisfies $m_-\ge m_+$, with equality precisely for the two-sided spatial Schwarzschild manifold. We also show that a strict gap between $c_{O,p}$ and the unconstrained capacity infimum $c_{M,p}$ detects a horizon.

</details>


### [108] [Holomorphic functions on complete Hermitian manifolds with flat Chern connection, II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10159)
*Duoxin Li,Hanyu Wu,Bo Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究完备陈平坦流形（陈曲率为零的完备Hermitian流形）的函数论，证明了其单连通情形下的椭圆性、多项式增长全纯函数维数估计，以及局部坐标条件下的双全纯分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作需假设挠率次线性增长，本文旨在去除或弱化该增长假设，在仅要求完备性与陈平坦的条件下推广相关函数论结果。

Method: 核心新分析工具是沿由平行酉标架生成的全纯流轨道的Cauchy估计版本，并结合Gromov椭圆性、多项式增长函数空间维数分析与双全纯分类论证。

Result: 证明完备单连通陈平坦流形在Gromov意义下是椭圆的；若挠率多项式增长，则双全纯于复Lie群；在无挠率增长假设下得到多项式增长全纯函数的尖锐维数估计；若该类函数在某点构成局部全纯坐标系，则流形双全纯于复欧氏空间。

Conclusion: 结果在更弱假设下推广了先前关于挠率次线性增长的工作，表明Cauchy估计沿全纯流轨道的方法可有效处理无增长限制的完备陈平坦流形。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove several results concerning the function theory of complete Hermitian manifolds with vanishing Chern curvature, which we refer to as complete Chern-flat manifolds. First, we prove that any complete simply connected Chern-flat manifold is elliptic in the sense of Gromov. Moreover, it is biholomorphic to a complex Lie group if the torsion has polynomial growth. Next, we obtain sharp dimension estimates for holomorphic functions of polynomial growth on complete Chern-flat manifolds, with no growth assumption on the torsion. Finally, we prove that any complete Chern-flat manifold admitting polynomial-growth holomorphic functions that form a local holomorphic coordinate system at some point is biholomorphic to complex Euclidean space. These results generalize our previous work, in which sublinear growth of the torsion was assumed. The new analytic tool is a version of Cauchy estimates along orbits of holomorphic flows generated by parallel unitary frames on such manifolds.

</details>


### [109] [Integral of Scalar Curvature under Volume Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10160)
*Guoyi Xu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在$\R^3$上构造了具有严格正Ricci曲率的光滑完备度量，其标量曲率的球平均积分渐近发散，从而否定地回答了Shing-Tung Yau关于渐近标量曲率积分的问题，并给出Naber局部猜想的紧流形反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shing-Tung Yau提出关于渐近标量曲率积分的问题，以及Naber提出的局部Shing-Tung Yau猜想，本文旨在给出这些问题的否定回答。

Method: 在$\R^3$上构造光滑完备度量，使其具有严格正Ricci曲率，并满足标量曲率的渐近积分发散条件；同时构造紧流形上的反例。

Result: 构造出的度量满足$\lim_{s\rightarrow\infty}s^{-1}\int_{B_p(s)}\Sc\,d\mu= \infty$；非紧例子具有零渐近体积比，紧反例的体积趋于零。

Conclusion: 该工作否定地回答了Shing-Tung Yau的渐近标量曲率积分问题，并给出Naber紧流形局部猜想的反例，表明在正Ricci曲率条件下相关猜想不成立。

Abstract: We construct a smooth complete metric on $\R^3$ with strictly positive Ricci curvature and $\displaystyle \lim_{s\rightarrow\infty}s^{-1}\int_{B_p(s)}\Sc\ddμ= \infty$. This gives a negative answer to Shing-Tung Yau's question on the asymptotic scalar-curvature integral. We also give counterexamples to Naber's local Shing-Tung Yau conjecture on compact manifolds. The noncompact example has zero asymptotic volume ratio, and the compact counterexamples have volumes tending to zero.

</details>


### [110] [Immortal Curve Shortening Flow on Analytic Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10197)
*Aprameya Girish Hebbar,Guanhua Shao,Qi Sun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在闭可定向实解析黎曼曲面上，任何光滑浸入的闭不朽曲线缩短流都收敛到唯一的非平凡闭测地线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲线缩短流在一般（非凸、非嵌入）初始条件下的长期渐近行为，特别是浸入曲线在解析曲面上的收敛性与极限的唯一性。

Method: 在闭可定向实解析黎曼曲面上，考虑光滑浸入的闭曲线缩短流，利用解析性克服奇点与退化问题，建立不朽曲线演化的收敛性论证。

Result: 证明了任何光滑浸入的闭不朽曲线缩短流均收敛，且极限是唯一的非平凡闭测地线。

Conclusion: 浸入曲线缩短流在实解析曲面上的长期行为完全由唯一的非平凡闭测地线刻画。

Abstract: We show that on any closed orientable real-analytic Riemannian surface, any smoothly immersed closed immortal curve-shortening flow converges to a unique non-constant closed geodesic.

</details>


### [111] [The measure contraction property on Grushin spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10208)
*Michael Albert,Samuël Borza*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文确定了径向Grushin空间G^{n+m}与α-Grushin平面的精确测度收缩指数,给出了其满足MCP(K,N)的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grushin型度量测度空间是亚黎曼几何中的重要模型,其测度收缩性质(measure contraction property, MCP)刻画了曲率-维数条件。此前经典的Grushin平面曲率指数已知为5,但高维径向情形与一般α情形的精确指数尚不清楚,且arXiv:2010.16350提出了相关猜想。

Method: 对径向Grushin空间(由X_i=∂_{x_i}、Y_j=|x|∂_{y_j}生成,配Lebesgue测度)及α-Grushin平面(由X=∂_x、Y_α=|x|^α∂_y生成)计算测度收缩指数;通过分析沿最优传输(位移插值)的Jacobian行列式增长率,并求解极值问题sup_{L>1} (2α+1)L/((L-1)^{2α+1}+1)来确定临界维数N_α。

Result: 证明了G^{n+m}满足MCP(K,N)当且仅当N≥n+4m且K≤0;对于α≥1,α-Grushin平面满足MCP(K,N)当且仅当K≤0且N≥N_α,其中N_α=1+max_{L>1}(2α+1)L/((L-1)^{2α+1}+1)。两结果在n=m=1与α=1时分别退化为经典Grushin平面的曲率指数5。

Conclusion: 本文解决了arXiv:2010.16350中的猜想,并对整数α≥2给出了首个具有非整数曲率指数的实解析亚黎曼结构的例子,统一并推广了经典Grushin平面曲率指数5的结果。

Abstract: We determine the sharp measure contraction exponents of two families of Grushin-type metric measure spaces. The radial Grushin space $\mathbb{G}^{n+m}$ is $\mathbb{R}^{n}\times\mathbb{R}^{m}$, equipped with Lebesgue measure and generated by $X_i=\partial_{x_i}$ and $Y_j=|x|\partial_{y_j}$, for $1\leq i\leq n$ and $1\leq j\leq m$. We prove that $\mathbb{G}^{n+m}$ satisfies $\operatorname{MCP}(K,N)$ if and only if $N\geq n+4m$ and $K\leq 0$. We also show that, for $α\geq1$, the $α$-Grushin plane generated by $X=\partial_x$ and $Y_α=|x|^α\partial_y$ satisfies $\operatorname{MCP}(K,N)$ if and only if $K\leq0$ and $N\geq N_α$, where \[ N_α:= 1+\max_{L>1} \frac{(2α+1)L}{(L-1)^{2α+1}+1}. \] This resolves the conjecture posed in arXiv:2010.16350 and, for integer $α\geq2$, provides the first examples of real-analytic sub-Riemannian structures with noninteger curvature exponent. Both results recover the known curvature exponent $5$ of the classical Grushin plane, corresponding respectively to $n=m=1$ and $α=1$.

</details>


### [112] [On a question by Firey concerning uniqueness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10238)
*Carlos Cabezas-Moreno*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明曲率方程在log-凹权重下唯一性（相差平移），肯定回答Firey唯一性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Firey曾提出关于曲率方程唯一性的问题，此前在C^2_+类中未解决，本文旨在给出肯定回答。

Method: 对闭C^2_+超曲面，考察曲率方程∑α_j E_j(τ_M)=∑α_j E_j(τ_N)，在(α_1,…,α_n)非负、非零、log-凹且无内部零点的条件下证明解的唯一性（相差平移）。

Result: 在给定log-凹、无内部零点的非负非零权重下，任意两个闭C^2_+超曲面若满足该曲率方程，则彼此仅相差一个平移。

Conclusion: 该结果在C^2_+类中肯定了Firey的唯一性问题，表明特定凸性权重可保证曲率方程的唯一性。

Abstract: We prove that the curvature equation $\sum_{j=1}^n α_j E_j(τ_{\mathcal{M}})=\sum_{j=1}^n α_j E_j(τ_{\mathcal{N}})$ yields uniqueness up to translation for any two closed $C^2_+$ hypersurfaces $\mathcal{M}, \mathcal{N}\hookrightarrow\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ whenever $(α_1,\ldots,α_n)\in\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}^n\setminus\{0\}$ is log-concave and has no internal zeros. This gives an affirmative answer to a uniqueness question posed by Firey in the $C^2_+$ class.

</details>


### [113] [Local Topology of Riemannian Manifolds with Lower Intermediate Ricci Curvature Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10268)
*Philipp Reiser,Masoumeh Zarei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对满足体积下界、直径上界与中间Ricci曲率下界的闭n维黎曼流形，证明了局部Betti数的消没定理，并推广至开流形得到全局Betti数消没定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在截面曲率与Ricci曲率下界情形下已有Betti数消没结果，作者希望找到一个介于两者之间的曲率条件（中间Ricci曲率），统一并插值这些已有结论。

Method: 在闭n维黎曼流形上，结合体积下界、直径上界与中间Ricci曲率下界，通过几何分析（曲率比较与局部化）论证局部Betti数的消没；方法进一步推广到开流形。

Result: 证明了在体积下界、直径上界和中间Ricci曲率下界条件下的局部Betti数消没定理，该结果推广并插值了截面曲率与Ricci曲率下界的已知结论；对开流形得到相应的全局Betti数消没定理。

Conclusion: 中间Ricci曲率可作为统一框架，同时覆盖截面曲率与Ricci曲率的消没现象，且适用于闭流形与开流形。

Abstract: We prove a vanishing theorem for the local Betti numbers of closed, $n$-dimensional Riemannian manifolds with a lower volume bound, an upper diameter bound, and a lower intermediate Ricci curvature bound. This generalizes and interpolates between known results under lower sectional and Ricci curvature bounds. Moreover, the methods extend to open manifolds, yielding a corresponding vanishing theorem for the global Betti numbers.

</details>


### [114] [Alexandrov-Type Rigidity for Minimal Capillary Hypersurfaces on Rotational Supports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10318)
*Caiyan Li,Chao Xia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在生成轮廓的适当条件下，单端旋转支撑面上的连通紧致嵌入极小毛细超曲面必为水平切片；并构造反例说明斜率条件不可省略，且在钝接触角下得到悬链面带的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究欧氏空间中受限于单端旋转超曲面的极小毛细超曲面的刚性（唯一性）问题，明确使"水平切片"成为唯一解的生成轮廓条件，并检验该条件的必要性。

Method: 在生成轮廓上施加适当的斜率/轮廓条件，证明连通、紧致、嵌入的极小毛细超曲面必为水平切片；通过构造光滑单端旋转支撑面承载非水平平面毛细 n-球，给出反例；对钝接触角附加反向轮廓不等式，进行悬链面带的分类。

Result: 证明了主定理：满足条件时唯一解为水平切片；构造出违反斜率条件时存在非水平平面毛细 n-球的反例；在钝接触角且反向轮廓不等式成立时，完成了 catenoid-band 的分类。

Conclusion: 水平切片的刚性依赖于生成轮廓的斜率条件，该条件在一般情况下不可去除；钝接触角情形下存在更为丰富的悬链面带状解，需以反向轮廓不等式刻画其分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that, under suitable conditions on the generating profile, every connected, compact embedded minimal capillary hypersurface supported on a one-ended rotational hypersurface in the Euclidean space is a horizontal slice. We also construct a smooth one-ended rotational support carrying a non-horizontal planar capillary $n$-ball, showing that the main slope condition cannot in general be omitted. For obtuse contact angles, we further obtain a catenoid-band classification under a reverse profile inequality.

</details>


### [115] [Unbounded normalized scalar curvature integrals in dimension four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10323)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在任意维度 $n\\geq4$ 上构造了 Yau 关于归一化标量曲率积分问题的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau 曾提出关于归一化标量曲率积分的问题，作者旨在对此给出反例，说明该问题在一般情形下不成立。

Method: 在每个维度 $n\\geq4$ 的 $\\mathbb{R}^n$ 上构造光滑完备、具有非负 Ricci 曲率且带极点的 Riemannian 度量 $g$，使得对任意固定点 $q$，$R^{2-n}\\int_{B_q(R)}\\mathrm{Scal}_g\\,\\mathrm{d}V_g$ 当 $R\\to\\infty$ 时趋于 $+\\infty$。

Result: 对所有 $n\\geq4$，均存在满足条件的度量，使归一化标量曲率积分发散到 $+\\infty$。

Conclusion: Yau 关于归一化标量曲率积分的问题在 $n\\geq4$ 的所有维度均被反例否定。

Abstract: We give counterexamples to Yau's question on normalized scalar curvature integrals in every dimension $n\geq4$. For each such $n$, there exists a smooth complete Riemannian metric $g$ on $\mathbb{R}^n$ with nonnegative Ricci curvature and a pole such that \[
  \lim_{R\to\infty}R^{2-n}\int_{B_q(R)}\mathrm{Scal}_g\,\mathrm dV_g=+\infty \] for every fixed $q\in\mathbb{R}^n$. Here $B_q(R)$ denotes the geodesic ball of radius $R$ centered at $q$, and $\mathrm{Scal}_g$ is the scalar curvature of $g$.

</details>


### [116] [Nonexistence of complete metrics with uniformly positive scalar curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10442)
*Tsz-Kiu Aaron Chow*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 论文证明：对万有覆盖为spin的连通可定向偶数维流形，若沿逃逸到无穷的紧集满足无限相对K-面积或相对上同调条件，则不存在具有一致正数量曲率的完备度量；证明用带边紧流形spin覆盖上的扭曲Dirac算子，结合基本域热核论证与长颈估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究偶数维、万有覆盖为spin的流形上一致正数量曲率（uniformly positive scalar curvature）完备度量的存在性障碍，将非紧流形的obstruction问题与相对K-面积及相对上同调条件联系起来。

Method: 设X为连通可定向偶数维流形，万有覆盖为spin。假设在某个紧补开子集中沿逃逸到无穷的紧集上成立无限相对K-面积或相对上同调条件。证明比较带边紧流形spin覆盖上的扭曲Dirac算子，结合基本域上的热核论证与长颈估计（long-neck estimate）。

Result: 若上述无限相对K-面积或相对上同调条件之一成立，则X不容许具有一致正数量曲率的完备度量。

Conclusion: 该工作给出非紧偶数维spin流形上一类一致正数量曲率完备度量的不存在性判据，扩展了相对K-面积与相对上同调型的obstruction方法。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a connected oriented even-dimensional manifold whose universal cover is spin. We prove that $X$ admits no complete metric with uniformly positive scalar curvature when either infinite relative $K$-area or a relative cohomological condition holds along compact sets escaping to infinity in a fixed open subset with compact complement. The proof compares twisted Dirac operators on spin covers of compact manifolds with boundary and combines a heat-kernel argument on fundamental domains with a long-neck estimate.

</details>


### [117] [Rigidity, sharp inequalities, and stability for $σ_2$-curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10523)
*Wangzhe Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用带可变参考曲率的经典共形散度恒等式，在半球面上证明四个关于共形度量的 $H_2$/$\\sigma_2$ 估计、刚性、分类与稳定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去除 Case-Wang 的 pinching 条件并推广若干已知刚性、积分不等式与稳定性结果到正 Einstein 背景及非增指定数据情形。

Method: 使用带可变参考曲率的经典共形散度恒等式。

Result: (1) 对圆半球上 $A_g\\in\\overline{\\Gamma_2^+}$ 且 $H_2$ 数据正的共形度量建立一般平均曲率估计；当 $\\sigma_2(A_g)=0$ 且边界数据非增时得到刚性，去除 $\\sup_\\Sigma H_g\\le 3\\inf_\\Sigma H_g$ 的 pinching 条件；$n\\ge5$ 常数据情形分类其 sharp $\\sigma_2$ Sobolev trace 猜想对应的光滑临界度量。(2) 在正 Einstein 共形类中，将 Viaclovsky 与 Gursky-Streets 的常 $\\sigma_2$ 刚性推广到非增指定数据，包括 $n\\ge5$ 且 Weyl 曲率非零的背景。(3) 将 Li-Li 的球面 $\\sigma_2/\\sigma_1$ 刚性与 Guan-Wang 的 sharp 积分不等式推广到正 Einstein 背景，后者在 $n\\ge5$ 正数量曲率下成立。(4) 将 Frank-Peteranderl 的球面 $\\sigma_2$ 稳定性推广到固定非球面正 Einstein 背景，$n\\ge5$，在正数量曲率下保持 $H^1$ 与 $W^{1,4}$ 控制。

Conclusion: 该抽象统一并推广了半球面共形几何中多重 $\\sigma_2$ 型结果，涵盖估计、刚性、分类、积分不等式和稳定性。

Abstract: Using classical conformal divergence identities with a variable reference curvature, we prove four main results. First, we establish a general mean-curvature estimate for conformal metrics on the round hemisphere with $A_g\in\overline{Γ_2^+}$ and positive prescribed $H_2$ data. When $σ_2(A_g)=0$ and the boundary data are nonincreasing, the estimate yields rigidity, removing Case-Wang's pinching condition $\sup_ΣH_g\le 3\inf_ΣH_g$. For $n\ge 5$, the constant-data case also classifies the smooth critical metrics associated with their sharp $σ_2$ Sobolev trace conjecture. Second, within positive Einstein conformal classes, we extend the constant-$σ_2$ rigidity results of Viaclovsky and Gursky-Streets to nonincreasing prescribed data, including backgrounds with nonzero Weyl curvature for $n\ge 5$. Third, we extend Li-Li's spherical $σ_2/σ_1$ rigidity and Guan-Wang's sharp integral inequality to positive Einstein backgrounds, with the latter holding for $n\ge 5$ under positive scalar curvature. Fourth, we extend Frank-Peteranderl's spherical $σ_2$ stability to fixed nonround positive Einstein backgrounds in dimensions $n\ge 5$, retaining $H^1$ and $W^{1,4}$ control under positive scalar curvature.

</details>


### [118] [A sharp threshold for mixed $Q$-curvature rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10527)
*Wangzhe Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了闭流形上由 Schouten 张量迹与 Branson Q-曲率线性组合 $I_a(g)=Q_g+a\sigma_2(A_g)$ 所定义的曲率量的刚性，给出尖锐阈值 $a\ge-4$，并通过径向射击构造说明阈值最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $I_a(g)=Q_g+a\sigma_2(A_g)$ 这类统一了 Q-曲率与 Schouten 张量二阶不变量的一般曲率量的刚性，确定其使得共形刚性成立的参数阈值，并刻画阈值的尖锐性。

Method: 结合逐点 Obata 恒等式与以标量曲率最小值为参考曲率的 Newton 恒等式证明刚性；利用局部极点方程与积分估计，通过径向射击构造把颈部与近球形帽相接，并使重标度颈部收敛到 Riemannian Schwarzschild 度量，确定颈部渐近尺度。

Result: 在 $n\ge4$ 维闭连通正 Einstein 流形 $(M,g_0)$ 上，对任意与 $g_0$ 共形、标量曲率非负且 $I_a(g)$ 为常数的光滑度量，当 $a\ge-4$ 时为 Einstein；该阈值逐维尖锐：对每个足够小的 $\eta>0$，球面 $S^n$ 上存在具有正标量曲率与常数 $I_{-4-\eta}(g)$ 的非 Einstein 共形度量。此外证明 $R_g>0,a\ge-4,\theta\le1$ 时 $I_a(g)=\Lambda R_g^\theta$ 的刚性；四维情形下无需标量曲率符号假设，$-4\le a\le-4/3$ 时常数-$I_a(g)$ 刚性成立。

Conclusion: $I_a(g)$ 常数化的共形刚性在 $a\ge-4$ 时成立且阈值最优，与四维 Einstein 度量共形刚性结果相衔接，并推广到带标量曲率幂的方程及四维更宽参数区间。

Abstract: Let $I_a(g)=Q_g+aσ_2(A_g)$, where $A_g$ is the Schouten tensor and $Q_g$ is Branson's $Q$-curvature. On a closed connected manifold of dimension $n\ge4$ with a positive Einstein metric $g_0$, we prove that every smooth metric conformal to $g_0$ with nonnegative scalar curvature and constant $I_a(g)$ is Einstein for $a\ge-4$. This lower threshold is sharp in every dimension: for each sufficiently small $η>0$, the round sphere admits a smooth non-Einstein conformal metric with positive scalar curvature and constant $I_{-4-η}(g)$. The rigidity proof combines the pointwise Obata identity with a Newton identity whose reference curvature is the minimum of the scalar curvature. A local pole equation and integral estimates yield a radial shooting construction joining a neck to nearly round caps. The rescaled necks converge to the Riemannian Schwarzschild metric, and we determine the asymptotic neck scale. We also prove rigidity for $I_a(g)=ΛR_g^θ$ when $R_g>0$, $a\ge-4$, and $θ\le1$. In dimension four, constant-$I_a(g)$ rigidity holds for $-4\le a\le-4/3$ without a scalar-curvature sign assumption.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Inverse Heat Source Problems from Boundary Flux and Interior Observations on Sets of Low Hausdorff Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10071)
*Ze Li,Zhiyuan Li,Zhi Zhou*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文研究有界 $C^{1,1}$ 区域上热方程逆源问题的条件稳定性，观测集为低 Hausdorff 维数集。基于新的 Dirichlet Laplacian 边界谱不等式，分别给出全时段与延迟/终端时段边界通量观测下的对数、双对数及 Hölder 稳定性，并覆盖内部观测情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典逆源问题稳定性多依赖全区域或高维观测，而实际测量常局限于低维集（如边界薄集）。作者旨在刻画在低 Hausdorff 维数观测集下，能否仍获得条件稳定性及其定量形式，并厘清维数阈值与观测时间对稳定性的影响。

Method: 针对时空可分离源（已知时间因子、未知空间分量），采用边界通量观测。核心工具是新的 Dirichlet Laplacian 边界谱不等式：用椭圆延拓法向导数在低维边界集上的观测控制有限 Dirichlet 谱和。据此推导全时段与延迟时段边界观测的条件稳定性；内部观测情形则结合谱 Gevrey 类与终端时刻观测。

Result: 边界观测：在正 $q$ 维 Hausdorff 容度的紧集上，全时段边界通量观测给出对数稳定性；延迟时段观测给出双对数稳定性。容许维数范围为：观测集位于平坦边界片时 $q>d-2$；一般 $C^{1,1}$ 边界时 $q>d-1-c_{d+1}$，其中 $c_{d+1}>0$ 仅依赖维数。内部观测：正 $q$ 维 Hausdorff 容度集（$q>d-1$）上，一般源 $H_0^1(\Omega)$ 全时段观测为对数稳定性；终端时刻观测在谱 Gevrey 类中为 Hölder 稳定性。

Conclusion: 低维观测集下逆热源问题仍具条件稳定性，但稳定性模依赖于观测时间与观测集几何/维数：延迟观测显著弱于全时段观测，平坦边界优于一般 $C^{1,1}$ 边界，源的正则性（Gevrey 类）可换取更强的 Hölder 稳定性。

Abstract: This paper investigates conditional stability for inverse source problems for the heat equation with a known temporal factor and an unknown spatial component in a bounded $C^{1,1}$ domain. We focus on observations supported on sets of low Hausdorff dimension and establish conditional stability in this setting. For boundary observations on compact sets of positive $q$-dimensional Hausdorff content, we establish logarithmic stability from full-time boundary flux observations and double-logarithmic stability from delayed-time boundary flux observations. The admissible dimensional ranges are $q>d-2$ when the observation set is contained in a flat boundary patch and $q>d-1-c_{d+1}$ on a general $C^{1,1}$ boundary, where $c_{d+1}>0$ depends only on the dimension. A key ingredient in deriving these results is a new boundary spectral inequality for the Dirichlet Laplacian, which controls a finite Dirichlet spectral sum through observations of the normal derivative of its elliptic extension on such a boundary set. Our results also cover inverse heat source problems with interior observations on sets of positive $q$-dimensional Hausdorff content for some $q>d-1$, yielding logarithmic stability from full-time observations for general sources in $H_0^1(Ω)$ and Hölder stability from terminal-time observations for sources in a suitable spectral Gevrey class.

</details>


### [120] [Resolvent bounds and eigenvalue estimates of generalized Schrödinger operators with complex potentials on compact manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10127)
*Eduard Stefanescu*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 将 Cuenin 关于复势 Schrödinger 算子的紧流形谱界推广到一般伪微分框架，核心是建立谱聚簇估计到复曲线上 L^p-L^{p'} 预解估计的"预解原理"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cuenin 的结果局限于紧流形上的 Schrödinger 算子，本文希望将其谱界推广至更一般的正自伴椭圆经典伪微分算子 P（正阶）与复值势 V 构成的算子 P+V，从而统一并扩展已有理论。

Method: 研究 P+V，其中 P 为正自伴椭圆经典伪微分算子、V 复值。核心分析工具是一个"预解原理"：证明 P 的谱聚簇估计蕴含沿合适复曲线的 L^p-L^{p'} 预解估计。再结合 Sogge 的谱聚簇界进行推导。

Result: 由预解原理与 Sogge 谱聚簇界得到外部区域的预解估计，推广了 Krupchyk–Uhlmann 的结果；同时在内部区域给出直接的预解界。在 Zoll 流形上讨论了所得谱界的尖锐性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个从谱聚簇估计到复曲线预解估计的通用机制，将复势谱界理论推广到伪微分设定，并讨论了 Zoll 流形情形下的最优性。

Abstract: We extend Cuenin's compact-manifold spectral bounds for Schrödinger operators with complex potentials to a general pseudodifferential setting. More precisely, we study operators \(P+V\), where \(P\) is a positive self-adjoint elliptic classical pseudodifferential operator of positive order and \(V\) is complex-valued.
  The main analytic input is a resolvent principle showing that spectral cluster estimates for \(P\) imply \(L^p\)-\(L^{p'}\) resolvent estimates along suitable complex curves. Combined with Sogge's spectral cluster bounds, this yields exterior-region resolvent estimates extending those of Krupchyk and Uhlmann; we also prove direct resolvent bounds in the interior region. On Zoll manifolds, we discuss the sharpness of the resulting spectral bounds.

</details>


### [121] [The sharp one-dimensional Lieb-Thirring inequality for the sum of eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10478)
*Larry Read*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 证明一维指数为1的Lieb-Thirring不等式的最佳常数为4/(3√3π),从而在该情形下证实了Lieb-Thirring猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lieb-Thirring不等式的最佳常数问题长期是数学物理领域的核心开放问题,指数为1的一维情形此前仅有猜想而无严格证明。

Method: 通过严格的分析论证(涉及变分/算子方法)确定一维指数为1时Lieb-Thirring不等式的最佳常数。

Result: 最佳常数为4/(3√3π)。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了一维指数为1情形下的Lieb-Thirring猜想。

Abstract: We prove that the best constant in the one-dimensional Lieb-Thirring inequality with exponent one is $4/(3\sqrt{3}π)$, confirming the Lieb-Thirring conjecture in this case.

</details>
