### [122] [Mockenhaupt's Three-Term Hardy-Littlewood Majorant Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09740)
*Guancheng Pan,Chengsong You,Hengyu Wang,Junwei Zhou,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对所有 $k \ge 4$ 的 Mockenhaupt 猜想，从而完整解决了所有 $k \ge 0$ 的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mockenhaupt 猜想断言：当 $2k < p < 2k+2$ 时，$\|g_k\|_{L^p(\mathbb{T})} > \|f_k\|_{L^p(\mathbb{T})}$，其中 $f_k = 1+e(x)+e((k+2)x)$、$g_k = 1+e(x)-e((k+2)x)$。此前仅对 $k \le 5$ 已知，$k \ge 6$ 的开放情形缺乏统一处理方法，作者希望给出对所有 $k$ 均成立的单一解析证明。

Method: 将范数比较归结为二维环面上的共振 Fourier 系数；经解析延拓后把这些系数表示为三重 Bessel 积分；借助 Neumann 乘积公式与 Weber–Schafheitlin 公式对主模态给出定量正下界，并用一致尾估计控制其余奇模态；最后证明主模态在每个 $k \ge 4$ 时支配尾部。

Result: 给出了对所有 $k \ge 4$ 成立的单一解析证明，解决了此前所有开放的 $k \ge 6$ 情形，并由此建立对所有 $k \ge 0$ 的猜想。

Conclusion: Mockenhaupt 猜想得到完整证明。论文指出其数学论证由自动研究系统 Apex Math 生成，完整 AI 使用声明见附录 A。

Abstract: For an integer $k \ge 0$, let $f_k(x) = 1 + e(x) + e((k+2)x)$ and $g_k(x) = 1 + e(x) - e((k+2)x)$ on $\mathbb{T} = \mathbb{R}/\mathbb{Z}$, where $e(x) = e^{2πi x}$. Mockenhaupt conjectured that $\|g_k\|_{L^p(\mathbb{T})} > \|f_k\|_{L^p(\mathbb{T})}$ whenever $2k < p < 2k+2$. The conjecture was previously known for $k \le 5$. We give a single analytic proof valid for every $k \ge 4$; in particular, this settles all previously open cases $k \ge 6$ and establishes the conjecture for every $k \ge 0$.
  The proof reduces the norm comparison to resonant Fourier coefficients on the two-torus and represents these coefficients, after analytic continuation, by triple-Bessel integrals. Neumann's product formula and the Weber-Schafheitlin formula yield a quantitative positive lower bound for the leading mode, while the remaining odd modes are controlled by a uniform tail estimate. The leading mode is then shown to dominate the tail for every $k \ge 4$.
  AI Usage. The mathematical argument of this paper was produced by the auto-research system Apex Math, an AI system built by Apex Intelligence. See Appendix A for the complete AI usage statement.

</details>


### [123] [Optimal dimension-dependent $\ell^p$ and $\ell^{1,\infty}$ estimates of the second-order discrete Riesz transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09770)
*Hanli Tang,Zewei Xu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了二阶离散Riesz变换的最优维数依赖估计，给出了非对角情形与对角差情形在ℓ^p和弱型(1,1)意义下的精确算子范数渐近式，并否定了Bañuelos-Kim的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定离散Riesz变换的精确维数依赖算子范数，并检验Bañuelos与Kim提出的关于离散Riesz变换ℓ^p有界性的猜想。

Method: 以c_d=Γ((d+2)/2)/π^{d/2}为归一化常数，直接计算二阶离散Riesz变换R_dis^{(jk)}f(n)的ℓ^p→ℓ^p与ℓ^1→ℓ^{1,∞}算子范数，并利用Stirling公式分析c_d的维数渐近行为。

Result: 对j≠k，任意1<p<∞有‖R_dis^{(jk)}‖_{ℓ^p→ℓ^p}=c_d[2/2^{d/2}+(8/3+o(1))d/3^{d/2}]，且2c_d/2^{d/2}≤‖R_dis^{(jk)}‖_{ℓ^1→ℓ^{1,∞}}≤c_d[2/2^{d/2}+(8/3+o(1))d/3^{d/2}]；对角情形R_dis^{(jj)}在ℓ^p→ℓ^p与弱型(1,1)均无界;而R_dis^{(jj-kk)}恢复抵消性，范数为4c_d[1+(1+o(1))d/2^{d/2}]，弱型(1,1)亦有对应的上下界。

Conclusion: 离散Riesz变换的ℓ^p估计随维数指数增长，从而对Bañuelos-Kim的猜想给出否定回答;对角核缺乏抵消性导致无界，作差后抵消性恢复并得到精确范数渐近。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate the optimal dimension-dependent estimates of the second-order discrete Riesz transforms \[ R_{\mathrm{dis}}^{(jk)}f(n) =
c_d\sum_{m\in\mathbb Z^d\setminus\{0\}} \frac{m_jm_k}{|m|^{d+2}}f(n-m), \qquad c_d=\frac{Γ\left(\frac{d+2}{2}\right)}{π^{d/2}}. \]
  For $j\neq k$ and every fixed $1<p<\infty$, we prove that \[ \|R_{\mathrm{dis}}^{(jk)}\|_{\ell^p\to\ell^p} =
c_d\left[ \frac{2}{2^{d/2}} +
\left(\frac83+o(1)\right)\frac{d}{3^{d/2}} \right] \] and \[ \frac{2c_d}{2^{d/2}} \leq \|R_{\mathrm{dis}}^{(jk)}\|_{\ell^1\to\ell^{1,\infty}} \leq c_d\left[ \frac{2}{2^{d/2}} +
\left(\frac83+o(1)\right)\frac{d}{3^{d/2}} \right]. \] Since $
c_d\sim \sqrt{πd}\left(\frac{d}{2πe}\right)^{d/2} $ by Stirling's formula, thl $\ell^p$ estimates give a negative answer to the conjecture proposed by Bañuelos and Kim in \cite{BK2}.
  The diagonal case exhibits quite a different phenomenon: for every $j$ and every $1<p<\infty$, $R_{\mathrm{dis}}^{(jj)}$ is neither bounded from $\ell^p(\mathbb Z^d)$ to $\ell^p(\mathbb Z^d)$ nor of weak type $(1,1)$. Cancellation is restored for the operators $R_{\mathrm{dis}}^{(jj-kk)} =R_{\mathrm{dis}}^{(jj)}-R_{\mathrm{dis}}^{(kk)}$. For every fixed $1<p<\infty$, \[ \|R_{\mathrm{dis}}^{(jj-kk)}\|_{\ell^p\to\ell^p} =
4c_d\left[ 1+(1+o(1))\frac{d}{2^{d/2}} \right], \] and \[ 4c_d \leq \|R_{\mathrm{dis}}^{(jj-kk)}\|_{\ell^1\to\ell^{1,\infty}} \leq 4c_d\left[ 1+(1+o(1))\frac{d}{2^{d/2}} \right]. \]

</details>


### [124] [Dimension-free estimates for the full discrete Euclidean ball maximal function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10083)
*Sheng-Chen Mao*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明 $\mathbb{Z}^d$ 上欧氏球平均的全极大算子在 $\ell^p(\mathbb{Z}^d)$（$1<p\le\infty$）上有界，且常数与维数无关，从而解决了 Stein 在 1990 年代中期提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 E.M. Stein 约 1990 年代中期提出的公开问题：离散欧氏球平均的全极大算子能否在 $\ell^p(\mathbb{Z}^d)$ 上一致有界（常数不依赖于维数 $d$）。

Method: 核心工具是乘子 $\mathfrak{m}_{\sqrt{\lfloor t^2\rfloor}}(\xi)$ 的渐近展开：当 $t\lesssim d$ 且 $t$ 充分大时，该乘子存在任意给定阶数的渐近展开，且关于 $\xi$ 一致；由此产生的极大算子可由 Mirek–Szarek–Wróbel 研究的离散规范化 Gaussian 极大函数控制。

Result: 证明了 $f\mapsto\sup_{t\ge0}|M_t f|$ 在 $\ell^p(\mathbb{Z}^d)$ 上有界，$1<p\le\infty$，且界常数与维数 $d$ 无关。

Conclusion: 建立了一致于维数的离散极大不等式，正面回答了 Stein 的问题，并揭示了乘子渐近展开与离散 Gaussian 极大函数之间的控制关系。

Abstract: Let $M_t$ denote the normalized average over the lattice points in the Euclidean ball of radius $t$ in $\mathbb{Z}^d$. We prove that the full maximal operator $f\mapsto\sup_{t\geq0}\lvert M_t f\rvert$ is bounded on $\ell^p(\mathbb{Z}^d)$, for every $1<p\leq\infty$, with a constant independent of the dimension. In particular, this resolves a question of E.M. Stein from the mid 1990s. The principal ingredient in our proof is that, when $t\lesssim d$ with $t$ sufficiently large, the associated multiplier $\mathfrak{m}_{\sqrt{\lfloor t^2\rfloor}}(ξ)$ admits an asymptotic expansion of arbitrary prescribed order, uniform in $ξ$, whose resulting maximal operators can be controlled by the discrete normalized Gaussian maximal function studied by Mirek--Szarek--Wróbel \cite{MSW25}.

</details>


### [125] [Loomis-Whitney inequalities on Reiter-Heisenberg groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10091)
*Sheng-Chen Mao,Ye Zhang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该论文为Reiter-Heisenberg群$\mathbb{G}_{qp}$证明了Loomis-Whitney不等式，并由此导出几何投影不等式、Gagliardo-Nirenberg-Sobolev不等式和等周不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Loomis-Whitney不等式是欧氏空间中的重要几何不等式，其在Carnot群等非欧结构上的推广具有理论价值；Reiter-Heisenberg群$\mathbb{G}_{qp}$是包含Heisenberg群（$q=1$）在内的一类二步Carnot群，为这类不等式提供了自然的推广框架。

Method: 证明基于Brascamp-Lieb不等式与熵次可加性之间的对偶关系：首先利用条件熵以及微分熵在保体积微分同胚下的不变性，从已知的一维Heisenberg群不等式推出$\mathbb{G}_{q1}$的情形；再通过有限中心和下Loomis-Whitney不等式的稳定性原理，从$\mathbb{G}_{q1}$过渡到$\mathbb{G}_{qp}$，该稳定性原理推广了(Zhang, 2024 arXiv:2402.02749v2)中的论证。

Result: 建立了Reiter-Heisenberg群$\mathbb{G}_{qp}$上的Loomis-Whitney不等式，并作为推论得到相应的几何投影不等式、Gagliardo-Nirenberg-Sobolev不等式以及等周不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作将Loomis-Whitney不等式从Heisenberg群推广到更一般的Reiter-Heisenberg群$\mathbb{G}_{qp}$，所采用的熵方法及中心和稳定性原理为Carnot群上几何与分析不等式的建立提供了可推广的技术途径。

Abstract: We establish a Loomis-Whitney inequality for the Reiter-Heisenberg groups $\mathbb{G}_{qp}$, a family of step-two Carnot groups that includes the Heisenberg groups when $q=1$. The proof is based on the duality between Brascamp-Lieb inequalities and entropy subadditivity: we first derive the result for $\mathbb{G}_{q1}$ from the known inequality on the first Heisenberg group, using conditional entropy and the invariance of differential entropy under volume-preserving diffeomorphisms; then we pass from $\mathbb{G}_{q1}$ to $\mathbb{G}_{qp}$ via a stability principle for Loomis-Whitney inequalities under finite central sums, which generalizes the argument in (Zhang, 2024 arXiv:2402.02749v2). As consequences, we obtain the associated geometric projection inequality, a Gagliardo-Nirenberg-Sobolev inequality, and an isoperimetric inequality.

</details>


### [126] [A proof of Askey's convexity conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10209)
*K. Castillo,S. Yakubovich*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 证明 Bessel 函数首类零点导出的函数 β(α) 在 (-1,1/2] 上严格凸，强化了 Askey 1993 凸性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Askey 于 1993 年提出相关凸性猜想，本文旨在强化并证明该猜想，给出 β(α) 的严格凸性结果。

Method: 利用原函数的正展开与加权和消失性质；相邻权重比值递增给出负协方差项，一次比较控制斜率与曲率；剩余代数步骤通过有限精确多项式计算证明八个有理不等式（附录给出）。

Result: 证明 β''(α)>0 对 -1<α≤1/2 成立（含 α=1/2 处左二阶导）；连续延拓 β(-1)=0 使 β 在 [-1,1/2] 上严格凸。

Conclusion: β 在 [-1,1/2] 上严格凸，强化了 Askey 1993 的凸性猜想，验证了该猜想。

Abstract: For $-1<α\leq1/2$, let $J_α$ be the Bessel function of the first kind and let $j_{α,2}$ be its second positive zero. Define $β(α)<α+1$ by $$ \int_0^{j_{α,2}}u^{-β(α)}J_α(u)\,du=0. $$ We prove that $β''(α)>0$ for $-1<α\leq1/2$, including the left second derivative at $α=1/2$. The continuous extension $β(-1)=0$ is strictly convex on $[-1,1/2]$, strengthening Askey's 1993 convexity conjecture. The analytic argument uses a positive expansion of a primitive and a vanishing weighted sum. Increasing ratios of consecutive weights give a negative covariance term, while one comparison controls slope and curvature. The remaining algebraic step proves eight rational inequalities by finite exact polynomial calculations, reproduced in an appendix.

</details>


### [127] [Positivity and Asymptotics for Chenevier's Orthogonal Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10328)
*Shisong Xu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Chenevier关于自守Hermite–Minkowski定理无条件部分中临界向量严格正性的猜想，核心是建立与权(arcsin x)/x相关圆测度的Verblunsky系数全严格负性，并给出临界向量弱收敛、精确一阶变分公式及相关渐近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chenevier在其自守Hermite–Minkowski定理的无条件部分中提出了临界向量严格正性猜想，该问题此前未被解决；同时该问题与圆测度的Verblunsky系数符号、Schur函数及Legendre测度扰动等经典正交多项式/矩问题工具存在深刻联系，值得从这些角度给出证明并揭示更精细的解析结构。

Method: 证明权(arcsin x)/x在(-1,1)上相关圆测度的所有Verblunsky系数严格负；使用正核公式与经典Schur算法确定符号，并通过para-orthogonal变换得到各次数的正性；进一步计算表示Schur函数负值的正密度（作为Hausdorff矩母函数）；对临界向量指标重标度至[0,1]研究其弱极限；对Legendre测度的指数扰动建立精确一阶变分公式并推导端点尖点渐近。

Result: 证明了Chenevier的严格正性猜想；建立了Verblunsky系数全严格负性及各次数正性；给出正密度的Hausdorff矩母函数表示；临界向量重标度后弱收敛于反正弦律，且Chenevier临界尺度渐近为8π/n；在临界边界上，唯一非零有效整向量恰好对奇数次数和零次数为负；对Legendre测度指数扰动得到精确一阶变分公式及其端点尖点渐近，其中通往Chenevier权的扰动在Legendre测度处导数含(log n)/(π²n²)项并在n^{-2}阶有显式常数。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了Chenevier的严格正性猜想，并通过Verblunsky系数、Schur算法、para-orthogonal变换、弱收敛与变分公式等工具揭示了该问题的解析与渐近机制；临界尺度、反正弦极限律及Legendre测度扰动的一阶渐近得到精确刻画，而对应的非线性渐近仍为猜想，留待后续研究。

Abstract: We prove the strict positivity conjectured by Chenevier for the critical vectors in the unconditional part of his automorphic Hermite--Minkowski theorem. The proof establishes strict negativity of all Verblunsky coefficients of a circle measure associated with the weight $(\arcsin x)/x$ on $(-1,1)$. A positive-kernel formula and the classical Schur algorithm give these signs, and a para-orthogonal transformation yields positivity in every degree. We also compute the positive density representing the negative of the Schur function as a Hausdorff moment generating function. After rescaling their indices to $[0,1]$, the normalized critical vectors converge weakly to the arcsine law, while Chenevier's critical scale is asymptotic to $8π/n$. At the critical boundary, a single nonzero effective integral vector is negative for every admissible test function exactly in odd degree and in degree zero. Finally, we prove an exact first-variation formula for exponential perturbations of the Legendre measure and derive its asymptotics for endpoint cusps. For the perturbation leading to Chenevier's weight, the derivative at the Legendre measure has a $(\log n)/(π^2n^2)$ term and an explicit constant at order $n^{-2}$. The corresponding nonlinear asymptotic remains conjectural.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Schur tests and estimates for positive semigroups on non commutative $L_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09339)
*Clément Coine,Mélina Laforge,Éric Ricard*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文将Arnold关于L_p空间上Kreiss正半群的估计推广到非交换情形（1<p<∞），给出反例说明估计的尖锐性，核心工具是Schur检验及其von Neumann代数版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arnold近期在L_p空间上给出了Kreiss正半群的估计结果，但仅限于交换（经典）情形。作者希望将该理论推广至非交换框架（von Neumann代数上的非交换L_p空间），并检验所得估计是否最优。

Method: 采用Schur检验（Schur test）作为主要技术工具，并为此发展了适用于von Neumann代数的新版Schur检验；在此基础上将Arnold的Kreiss正半群估计从交换L_p空间推广到非交换L_p空间。

Result: 在非交换L_p空间（1<p<∞）上建立了Kreiss正半群的相应估计，并构造了一个尖锐（sharp）的例子，表明所得估计在常数或阶数意义上无法进一步改进。

Conclusion: Arnold的Kreiss正半群L_p估计可有效推广到非交换情形，von Neumann代数上的Schur检验是实现该推广的关键工具，且所获结果具有尖锐性。

Abstract: We extend the recent results of Arnold on estimates for Kreiss positive semigroups on $L_p$-spaces to the non commutative setting, $1<p<\infty$. We also provide a sharp example. All the results are achieved thanks to the use of the Schur test, including a new version for von Neumann algebras.

</details>


### [129] [A discrete Mellin calculus in the Toeplitz algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09453)
*Carlo Bellavita,Georgios Stylogiannis*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明经典Cesàro算子属于Toeplitz代数，独立解决了Barría–Halmos问题。为此建立采样比矩阵W(κ)的离散Mellin演算：该类量化模Hilbert–Schmidt算子保持乘法性，且每个W(κ)属于Toeplitz代数的换位子理想；Cesàro算子对应核κ=1_(0,1]，故为特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barría与Halmos提出经典Cesàro算子是否属于Toeplitz代数的问题。此前缺乏直接、独立的证明，本文旨在给出新的解法并将其纳入更一般的框架。

Method: 引入采样比矩阵 W(κ)_{jk}=1/(j+1)·κ((k+1)/(j+1))，对一类自然核代数A建立离散Mellin演算。证明该量化模Hilbert–Schmidt算子S_2是乘性的：W(κ)W(η)−W(κ⋆η)属于S_2，其中⋆为核的卷积；并证明每个W(κ)(κ∈A)属于Toeplitz代数的换位子理想。

Result: 证明了量化模Hilbert–Schmidt的乘法公式；证明所有W(κ)均落在Toeplitz代数的换位子理想中。由于Cesàro算子对应核κ=1_(0,1]，Barría–Halmos问题作为该一般框架的特例得到肯定解决。

Conclusion: 通过离散Mellin演算与采样比矩阵量化，本文独立地证明了Cesàro算子属于Toeplitz代数，并将结论推广到一类核所定义的算子族，统一处理了Barría–Halmos问题。

Abstract: We prove that the classical Cesàro operator belongs to the Toeplitz algebra, providing an independent solution to a question raised by Barría and Halmos. Our approach is based on a discrete Mellin calculus for the sampled-ratio matrices \[
  W(κ)_{jk} = \frac{1}{j+1}\, κ\!\left(\frac{k+1}{j+1}\right). \] For a natural algebra of kernels $A$, we prove that this quantization is multiplicative modulo Hilbert--Schmidt operators, \[
  W(κ)W(η) - W(κ\star η) \in S_2,
  \qquad κ,η\in A. \] We further show that every operator $W(κ)$, $κ\in A$, belongs to the commutator ideal of the Toeplitz algebra. Since the Cesàro operator corresponds to the kernel $κ= \mathbf 1_{(0,1]}$, this resolves the Barría--Halmos question as a special case of the general framework.

</details>


### [130] [Explicit Full-Spark and Quantitatively Phase-Retrievable Gabor Windows via Algebraic Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09614)
*Dongwei Li*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文构造了显式有限Gabor窗，使其轨道同时具备full spark与相位可恢复性，并给出其模糊裕度的尖锐阶估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限幂零群相位恢复中显式同时构造问题，即寻找轨道同时full spark且相位可恢复的显式Gabor窗。

Method: 通过代数特化保持所有Gabor子式并消除自模糊函数零点；核心是代数正则化原理：充分小的代数扰动从一个种子转移full spark，同时从另一个种子保留模糊间隙；并用中国剩余张量化推广到无平方因子循环维数。

Result: 每个素数维p≥5给出显式full spark窗，归一化模糊裕度至少c0 p^{-1/2}（阶尖锐，由有限Moyal恒等式）；推广至有界素因子个数的无平方因子循环维数达N^{-1/2}尺度；第二个种子给出O(N^{-2})的多项式模糊裕度并在几何种子构造内尖锐；未修改的三次chirp在每个模p^a（a≥2）处有系统模糊零点；p≥2^14时在固定两原子Gabor子空间上对固定比例任意擦除具一致稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了full spark与相位可恢复性同时成立的显式构造，刻画了模糊裕度的尖锐阶，并指出三次chirp方法的边界，其擦除稳定性结论限于单个两原子子空间。

Abstract: We construct explicit finite Gabor windows whose orbits are simultaneously full spark and phase retrievable. In every cyclic dimension, algebraic specialization preserves all Gabor minors while removing every zero of the self-ambiguity function, thereby resolving the explicit simultaneous- construction problem arising in finite nilpotent-group phase retrieval. We isolate the underlying regularization principle: a sufficiently small algebraic perturbation transfers full spark from one seed while retaining an ambiguity gap from another. In every prime dimension \(p\geq5\), this gives an explicit full-spark window with normalized ambiguity margin at least \(c_0p^{-1/2}\), for an absolute constant \(c_0>0\); the order is sharp by the finite Moyal identity. Chinese-remainder tensorization followed by the same regularization extends the \(N^{-1/2}\) scale to squarefree cyclic dimensions having a bounded number of prime factors. The resulting full-data lifted intensity map has a dimension-independent lower stability bound on each such family. A second explicit seed gives a polynomial ambiguity margin, of order \(N^{-2}\), in every cyclic dimension, and we prove that this order is sharp within the geometric-seed construction. We also identify why the sharper cubic-chirp argument itself cannot cross that boundary: the unmodified cubic chirp has systematic ambiguity zeros at every modulus \(p^a\), \(a\geq2\). For a smaller fixed character perturbation and \(p\geq2^{14}\), we additionally prove uniform stability after a fixed fraction of arbitrary erasures on each fixed two-atom Gabor subspace. This last statement is deliberately local to one two-atom subspace and does not compare signals supported on different pairs.

</details>


### [131] [Sharp Conditioning for Matrix Recovery by Finite Affine Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09619)
*Dongwei Li*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究仿射群在标准(q-1)维不可约表示下秩一轨道的定性矩阵恢复，对显式实生成窗给出尖锐的最小奇异值下界，并刻画最优窗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定性矩阵恢复虽已知，但缺乏对显式实生成窗最小奇异值的尖锐下界与最优性刻画，需要确定条件数与最优窗结构。

Method: 计算非负双层窗的精确最小奇异值并识别唯一最优；对q=3^h构造三迹类绝对迹窗；将全局问题归约为四个标量表达式与对称2×2矩阵；结合平方和恒等式、一致二次型证书与有限几何块分解。

Result: 给出任意非负双层窗的精确最小奇异值，h≥2且q-1≥10时唯一最优；条件数在每个固定奇特征塔下有界且仅差显式特征相关因子；构造的迹窗最小奇异值为q(√2−1)/(q(2−√2)−1)>1/√2，并证明其在三迹类实窗中唯一最优（至全局符号与两非零迹类互换）。

Conclusion: 实生成窗的最小奇异值具有尖锐刻画与唯一最优结构，零迹均值是全局最优性的推论。

Abstract: Let \(q=p^h\) be an odd prime power, and let the affine group \(\mathbb F_q\rtimes\mathbb F_q^\times\) act through its canonical \((q-1)\)-dimensional irreducible representation. Qualitative matrix recovery for these rank-one orbits is known. We determine sharp lower singular-value bounds for explicit real generating windows. First, we compute the exact least singular value for every nonnegative two-level window over \(\mathbb F_{p^h}\) with \(h\geq2\), and identify the unique optimizer when \(q-1\geq10\). The resulting conditioning stays bounded away from zero on every fixed odd-characteristic tower and is within an explicit characteristic-dependent factor of the best possible value over all real windows.
  For every \(q=3^h\), \(h\geq2\), we construct a real three-level absolute-trace window (constant on the fibers of \(\operatorname{Tr}_{\mathbb F_q/\mathbb F_3}\)) whose least singular value is \[
  \frac{q(\sqrt2-1)}{q(2-\sqrt2)-1}>\frac1{\sqrt2}. \] For the full class of real windows constant on the three trace classes, we reduce the least singular value to four scalar expressions and a symmetric \(2\times2\) matrix. This yields the exact global optimum and all equality cases: the displayed trace window is uniquely optimal up to global sign and interchange of the two nonzero trace classes. Thus zero trace mean follows from optimality. The proof combines an explicit sum-of-squares identity, uniform quadratic-form certificates, and a finite-geometric block decomposition of the orbit measurement operator.

</details>


### [132] [Power quasinormal operators and the root problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09775)
*Jing-Bin Zhou,Shihai Yang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个n次幂拟正规但T^n非拟正规的算子反例，修正了已有文献中的引理错误，并系统研究了n次幂拟正规性、T^n正规性与T^n拟正规性之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献\cite[Lemma 3.1]{ko-filomat-2023}被后续多个结论（如n次幂拟正规算子具有单值延拓性质）所依赖，但该引理本身存在错误。本文旨在通过构造反例揭示这一漏洞，并给出不依赖该引理的独立证明，从而弥补原始论证中的缺口，同时推广相关结果并解决公开问题。

Method: 构造满足T为n次幂拟正规但T^n非拟正规的具体算子作为反例；进而分析T的n次幂拟正规性、T^n的正规性与T^n的拟正规性三者的逻辑关系；给出n次幂拟正规算子具有单值延拓性质的新证明；对固定算子T刻画使其为n次幂拟正规的所有正整数n；并证明每个亚正规的n次幂拟正规算子均为拟正规。

Result: 给出了反例推翻\cite[Lemma 3.1]{ko-filomat-2023}；证明在有限维空间中n次幂拟正规性、T^n正规性与T^n拟正规性三者等价；独立证明n次幂拟正规算子具有单值延拓性质；刻画了使T为n次幂拟正规的正整数n集合；推广了Sid Ahmed \cite{ahmed-bmaa-2011}的若干结论；证明了亚正规的n次幂拟正规算子必为拟正规，并对Stanković与Kubrusly \cite[Question 2.11]{stankovic-afa-2025}的根问题给出肯定回答。

Conclusion: 该工作纠正了既有文献的关键引理错误，厘清了n次幂拟正规算子相关性质间的等价关系，扩展了经典结果，并解决了两个公开问题，对算子理论中拟正规性与单值延拓性质的研究具有推进意义。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct an $n$-power quasinormal operator $T$ such that $T^n$ is not quasinormal for some positive integer $n$, thereby providing a counterexample to \cite[Lemma 3.1]{ko-filomat-2023}. We then investigate the relationships among the $n$-power quasinormality of $T$, the normality of $T^n$, and the quasinormality of $T^n$, and show that these three conditions are equivalent in finite-dimensional spaces. We also provide a new proof that $n$-power quasinormal operators have the single-valued extension property \cite[Theorem 3.2]{ko-filomat-2023}; unlike the original proof, our argument does not rely on \cite[Lemma 3.1]{ko-filomat-2023} and thus closes the gap in the original argument. In addition, for a fixed operator $T$, we characterize all positive integers $n$ for which $T$ is $n$-power quasinormal. As consequences, several results of Sid Ahmed \cite{ahmed-bmaa-2011} are extended. Finally, we prove that every paranormal $n$-power quasinormal operator is quasinormal. Closely related to this, we also give an affirmative answer to the root problem of Stanković and Kubrusly \cite[Question 2.11]{stankovic-afa-2025}.

</details>


### [133] [Sharpness Loss for Radon--Nikodym Derivatives of Completely Positive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09915)
*Mohsen Kian*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究投影值Radon–Nikodym导数（sharp子映射）在复合下的行为，证明sharp性损失由诱导拉回映射的乘法缺陷决定；有限维情形下该缺陷由复合Kraus关系空间的正交补 $\mathcal{E}_{\Lambda,\Omega}$ 刻画，并给出sharp性保持的充要条件、张量因子判据及损失缺陷的定量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全正映射的Radon–Nikodym定理将映射下方的序区间与其极小Stinespring表示交换子中的正压缩对应起来，但投影值Radon–Nikodym导数（sharp子映射）在复合运算下是否保持sharp性尚不清楚，这一结构性问题是本文的研究动机。

Method: 以完全正映射的Radon–Nikodym定理为框架，研究投影值Radon–Nikodym导数在复合下的行为；引入诱导拉回映射的乘法缺陷作为刻画工具；在有限维情形下利用复合Kraus关系空间及其正交补 $\mathcal{E}_{\Lambda,\Omega}$，并结合提升后的Radon–Nikodym投影的约化性质进行分析。

Result: 证明了sharp性损失由诱导拉回映射的乘法缺陷决定；在有限维情形下该缺陷由 $\mathcal{E}_{\Lambda,\Omega}$（复合Kraus关系空间的正交补）确定；一个sharp子映射保持sharp当且仅当 $\mathcal{E}_{\Lambda,\Omega}$ 约化提升的Radon–Nikodym投影；并建立了sharp性全局保持的张量因子判据，显式量化了sharp性损失缺陷。

Conclusion: 投影值Radon–Nikodym导数的复合sharp性可完全由拉回映射的乘法缺陷及有限维下的Kraus关系空间正交补刻画，所得约化条件与张量因子判据为sharp性损失提供了精确的结构性与定量描述。

Abstract: The Radon--Nikodym theorem for completely positive maps identifies the order interval below a map with positive contractions in the commutant of its minimal Stinespring representation. In this paper, we study the behavior of projection-valued Radon--Nikodym derivatives (sharp submaps) under composition. We prove that sharpness loss is determined by the multiplicative defect of the induced pullback map. In the finite-dimensional case, we show that this defect is determined by the orthogonal complement of the composite Kraus relation space, denoted $\mathcal{E}_{Λ,Ω}$. A sharp submap remains sharp if and only if $\mathcal{E}_{Λ,Ω}$ reduces the lifted Radon--Nikodym projection. Furthermore, we establish a tensor-factor criterion for the global preservation of sharpness and explicitly quantify the sharpness-loss defect.

</details>


### [134] [A Dimension-Independent Commutator Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09938)
*Hao Shen,Jiaqi Wang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明任意迹零矩阵 A 可写成 A=BC−CB，且 ∥B∥∥C∥ ≤ K∥A∥，K 为与 n 无关的绝对常数；在限定某因子为指定基下对角阵时，构造出迫使下界为 √(log n) 阶的零对角 Hermitian 酉矩阵族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究迹零矩阵的交换子表示 A=BC−CB 中范数乘积 ∥B∥∥C∥ 能否被独立于维数 n 的绝对常数控制，并区分「无限制」与「限定某因子为给定基下对角阵」两种情形下范数界的本质差异。

Method: 对固定 t>0，依是否对所有 θ 都有 ∥Re(e^{iθ}A)∥₁ ≥ tn∥A∥ 分情形：成立时直接构造交换子表示；不成立时用 Marcus–Spielman–Srivastava 的向量选择定理得到范数足够小的迹零压缩，使归纳得以闭合。另构造零对角 Hermitian 酉矩阵族给出 √(log n) 阶下界，并分析其 ε-铺砌与双正规因子表示。主要结果在 Lean 4/Mathlib 中形式化。

Result: 得到 ∥B∥∥C∥ ≤ K∥A∥ 的绝对常数界；构造出一族零对角 Hermitian 酉矩阵，当要求某因子在指定基下对角时，迫使 ∥B∥∥C∥ 下界为 √(log n) 阶；该族具有少于 2ε⁻² 块的 ε-铺砌，并可用两个正规因子表示且范数乘积最优为 1/2；同时在 Lean 4 中形式化了主结果并给出 Kadison–Singer 状态延拓定理的形式化推导。

Conclusion: 无限制交换子范数界与限定对角限制下的范数界存在本质区别：前者有绝对常数界，后者可被迫达到 √(log n) 阶下界。

Abstract: We prove that every trace-zero matrix $A\in M_n(\mathbb{C})$ admits a representation $A=BC-CB$ with $B,C\in M_n(\mathbb{C})$ and $\lVert B\rVert\lVert C\rVert\le K\lVert A\rVert$, where $K$ is an absolute constant independent of $n$, and $\lVert\cdot\rVert$ denotes the operator norm. For a fixed $t>0$, the proof splits according to whether $\lVert\operatorname{Re}(e^{\mathrm{i}θ}A)\rVert_1\ge tn\lVert A\rVert$ holds for all $θ\in\mathbb{R}$, where $\lVert\cdot\rVert_1$ denotes the trace norm. When this lower bound holds, we construct a commutator representation directly. Otherwise, the vector-selection theorem of Marcus, Spielman, and Srivastava yields smaller trace-zero compressions whose norms are small enough for the induction to close. We also construct an explicit family of zero-diagonal Hermitian unitaries that forces a lower bound of order $\sqrt{\log n}$ for $\lVert B\rVert\lVert C\rVert$ when either factor is required to be diagonal in the prescribed basis. The same family admits $\varepsilon$-pavings with fewer than $2\varepsilon^{-2}$ blocks and representations by two normal factors with optimal norm product $1/2$. This establishes a distinction between unrestricted commutator bounds and bounds under a prescribed diagonal restriction. The main results and their essential inputs are formalized in Lean 4 using Mathlib. The development also includes a formal derivation of the Kadison-Singer state-extension theorem from the same vector-selection theorem.

</details>


### [135] [Aubin--Lions Compactness for Stieltjes--Bochner Evolution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10064)
*Francisco J. Fernández*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文建立以 Lebesgue–Stieltjes 测度 μ_g 度量的 Banach 值微积分基本定理与 Aubin–Lions 紧性定理，允许绝对连续、奇异连续和原子分量；在 Radon–Nikodým 性质下刻画 g-绝对连续曲线，并证明状态投影为紧算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典微积分基本定理与 Aubin–Lions 紧性定理通常针对 Lebesgue 测度（绝对连续演化）。本文旨在将其推广到由非降左连续函数 g 诱导的 Lebesgue–Stieltjes 测度 μ_g，从而统一处理绝对连续、奇异连续与原子三类分量，特别关注终端原子导致导数在 Bochner 状态类中"不可见"的情形。

Method: 引入 g-绝对连续曲线与强 g-导数的概念，建立 Banach 值微积分基本定理：当值空间具 Radon–Nikodým 性质时，g-绝对连续等价于不定 Bochner 积分，强 g-导数为 Bochner 密度，变差测度密度等于其范数。为处理终端原子造成的导数不可见性，将自然演化空间构造为状态–导数对的图。紧性证明结合原子处的有界求值、非原子点处的局部 μ_g-平均以及 Ehrling 不等式，在 B_0⊂⊂B↪B_1 且 B_0、B_1 自反、1<p_0,p_1<∞ 的框架下证明状态投影为紧线性算子。

Result: 在 Radon–Nikodým 性质下得到 g-绝对连续的等价刻画；紧性定理表明状态投影是从演化空间到 L_g^{p_0}([a,b);B) 的紧线性算子。该结果可退化为经典定理，并导出有界时间尺度、加权序列与混合 Stieltjes 测度的紧性原理。

Conclusion: 本文为 Stieltjes 型演化提供了统一的 Banach 值微积分与紧性框架，覆盖连续、奇异连续与原子分量，并通过状态–导数对图结构解决原子处导数缺失问题；结果推广经典微积分基本定理与 Aubin–Lions 定理，并适用于多种离散与混合测度情形。

Abstract: Let $g$ be a nondecreasing left-continuous function and let $μ_g$ be its Lebesgue--Stieltjes measure. We establish a Banach-valued fundamental theorem of calculus and an Aubin--Lions compactness theorem for evolution measured by $μ_g$, allowing absolutely continuous, singular continuous and atomic components. If the range space has the Radon--Nikodým property, a curve is $g$-absolutely continuous if and only if it is an indefinite Bochner integral; its strong $g$-derivative is the Bochner density and the variation measure has density equal to its norm. A terminal atom may make the derivative invisible from the Bochner state class, so the natural evolution space is a graph of state--derivative pairs. For $B_0\Subset B\hookrightarrow B_1$, with $B_0$ and $B_1$ reflexive and $1<p_0,p_1<\infty$, the state projection is a compact linear operator into $L_g^{p_0}([a,b);B)$. The proof combines bounded evaluation at atoms, local $μ_g$-averages at nonatomic points and Ehrling's inequality. The result recovers the classical theorem and yields compactness principles for bounded time scales, weighted sequences and mixed Stieltjes measures.

</details>


### [136] [Whitney extension theorem and intrinsic rectifiability of jump sets in Carnot-Carathéodory spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10471)
*Marco Di Marco*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在等正则Carnot-Carathéodory空间上，局部可积函数的本质跳跃集是本质可数可求长的；关键工具为内在C^1函数的局部标量Whitney延拓定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典欧氏空间中，BV函数的跳跃集可数可求长已有成熟理论，但在次黎曼几何（Carnot-Carathéodory空间）中，缺乏适配内在度量的相应刻画，特别是如何描述跳跃集的可求长性尚不清楚。

Method: 在等正则Carnot-Carathéodory空间框架下，引入并证明内在C^1函数的局部标量Whitney延拓定理，以此作为核心工具，分析局部可积函数的本质跳跃集结构并证明其本质可数可求长性。

Result: 证明了等正则Carnot-Carathéodory空间上任意局部可积函数的本质跳跃集是本质可数可求长的。

Conclusion: 结果将欧氏空间中关于跳跃集可求长性的理论推广至次黎曼情形，表明内在几何下的跳跃集仍具有良好的可求长结构，且局部Whitney延拓定理是该类问题中的有效工具。

Abstract: We prove that the intrinsic jump set of every locally integrable function on an equiregular Carnot-Carathéodory space is intrinsically countably rectifiable. A key new ingredient is a local scalar Whitney extension theorem for intrinsic $C^1$ functions.

</details>


### [137] [Littlewood subordination for de Branges--Rovnyak spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10115)
*Emmanuel Fricain,Andreas Hartmann,William T. Ross,Dan Timotin*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究作用于不同 de Branges–Rovnyak 空间之间的复合算子，借鉴 Mashreghi–Shabankhah 关于模型空间间复合算子的工作，采用再生核 Hilbert 空间方法、Sarason 的积分算子观点及 Aleksandrov–Clark 测度，并回答了 Dellepiane–Seco 提出的若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究（Mashreghi–Shabankhah）针对模型空间之间的复合算子取得成果，而 de Branges–Rovnyak 空间作为模型空间的推广，其不同空间之间的复合算子尚缺乏系统刻画；同时 Dellepiane 与 Seco 提出的相关问题仍未解决。

Method: 采用再生核 Hilbert 空间方法，借助 Sarason 将复合算子视为积分算子的处理框架，并应用 Aleksandrov–Clark 测度作为分析工具。

Result: 给出了不同 de Branges–Rovnyak 空间之间复合算子的若干主要刻画与结论，并解答了 Dellepiane 与 Seco 提出的若干相关开放问题。

Conclusion: 该工作将模型空间上复合算子的理论推广至不同 de Branges–Rovnyak 空间的情形，验证了再生核与 Aleksandrov–Clark 测度方法在此类问题中的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study composition operators that act between different de Branges-Rovnyak spaces. Our main results are suggested by a paper of Mashreghi and Shabankhah concerning composition operators between model spaces. We also answer several related open questions posed by Dellepiane and Seco. To prove these results, we apply reproducing kernel Hilbert space methods, Sarason's approach to composition operators as integral operators, and Aleksandrov-Clark measures.

</details>


### [138] [Noncommutative sharp Hausdorff-Young inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10424)
*Hongsen Qiu,Xiao Xiong*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在量子欧几里得空间上证明了尖锐的Hausdorff–Young不等式，并由此导出Weyl变换与Heisenberg群上的尖锐Hausdorff–Young常数；核心工具是源于Gabor变换的新型算子值流，方法基于算子演算，具备一般适用性，最后给出非交换卷积定义及尖锐Young不等式的部分结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hausdorff–Young不等式在调和分析中具有基础地位，其在非交换框架（量子欧几里得空间、Weyl变换、Heisenberg群）上的尖锐常数形式尚待确立；作者希望借助算子演算提供一套可推广的方法来解决此类尖锐常数问题。

Method: 构造与Gabor变换相关的新型算子值流，并主要运用算子演算（operator calculus）进行证明；该框架使得方法可推广至类似问题。

Result: 证明了量子欧几里得空间上的尖锐Hausdorff–Young不等式；结果蕴含Weyl变换及Heisenberg群的尖锐Hausdorff–Young常数；给出了非交换卷积的定义，并获得尖锐Young不等式的部分结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将尖锐Hausdorff–Young理论拓展到量子欧几里得空间等非交换情形，并通过算子值流与算子演算提供了通用技术路线；非交换卷积与尖锐Young不等式仍留有部分开放问题。

Abstract: We prove the sharp Hausdorff--Young inequality on the quantum Euclidean space. Meanwhile, our result implies the sharp Hausdorff-Young constants for the Weyl transform, as well as that for the Heisenberg group. The key ingredient is a novel operator-valued flow related to the Gabor transform. Our method is mainly based on operator calculus and hence generally applicable to similar problems. Finally, we give the definition of noncommutative convolution and include the partial results for the sharp Young's inequality.

</details>


### [139] [The bounds $\hbar_{p,X}\lesssim(β_{p,X})^2$ and $β_{p,X}\lesssim(\hbar_{p,X})^2$ are sharp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10444)
*Emiel Lorist,Jan van Neerven*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明了Hilbert变换常数与UMD常数之间二次界估计的两个方向均最优，构造了两族显式Banach空间分别实现增长速率n与√n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burkholder和Bourgain在1980年代证明UMD_p性质等价于Hilbert变换在L^p(R;X)上的有界性，且相应常数的界为二次型。此二次界是否最优长期未明。

Method: 构造显式的有限维Banach空间（维数为2^n），分别设计使Hilbert变换常数与UMD常数呈现不同增长阶的两族例子。

Result: 第一族空间中Hilbert变换常数按n增长、UMD常数按√n增长；第二族空间呈现相反的behavior。由此表明β_{p,X}与ℏ_{p,X}之间的二次界两个方向均为sharp。

Conclusion: UMD常数与Hilbert变换常数之间二次界的两个不等式均不可改进，解决了关于界的最优性问题。

Abstract: It was proved in the 1980s by Burkholder and Bourgain that, for any Banach space $X$ and $1<p<\infty$, the UMD$_p$ property for $X$ is equivalent to boundedness of the Hilbert transform on $L^p(\R;X)$, and that the UMD constant $β_{p,X}$ and the Hilbert transform constant $\hbar_{p,X}$ are related by the quadratic bounds \begin{equation*}
  \hbar_{p,X}\lesssim(β_{p,X})^2,
  \qquad
  β_{p,X}\lesssim(\hbar_{p,X})^2. \end{equation*} In this paper we present examples showing that both bounds are sharp. More precisely, we construct explicit $2^n$-dimensional Banach spaces for which the Hilbert transform constant grows like $n$ and the UMD constant like $\sqrt n$, and a second family with the reverse behaviour.

</details>


### [140] [Local Reflexivity and Duality for Subspace Approximation Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10460)
*Daniel Akech Thiong*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究算子逼近中子空间逼近格式与局部自反性的关系，证明无限维可容许格式自然生成满足Oja-Veidenberg嵌套原理的完全嵌套，从而取消ELRP公理；有限维成分格式的双对偶几何崩塌。由此表明ELRP完全多余，逼近数的对偶关系对所有有界线性算子无条件成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于格式相对逼近数对偶性 a_n(T,Q)=a_n(T^{**},Q^{⊥⊥}) 的文献将扩展局部自反性(ELRP)作为独立公理假设，这是一个结构性缺口，作者希望厘清逼近格式与局部自反性之间的内在联系，判断ELRP是否必要。

Method: 采用二分法(dichotomy)分析：一方面对无限维可容许逼近格式，证明其自然生成完全嵌套(complete nests)，恰好满足Oja-Veidenberg局部自反性嵌套原理的拓扑假设；另一方面对有限维成分的逼近格式，分析其双对偶几何的退化行为。核心工具是初等的等距限制(isometric restriction)。

Result: 证明了二分性结果：无限维可容许格式自然满足Oja-Veidenberg嵌套原理的拓扑条件；而有限维成分格式的双对偶几何崩塌。据此得出ELRP作为独立公理是完全多余的。

Conclusion: 逼近数的对偶关系 a_n(T,Q)=a_n(T^{**},Q^{⊥⊥}) 无需ELRP假设，对所有有界线性算子无条件成立，可通过初等等距限制得到证明，从而完善了算子逼近的定量理论。

Abstract: We address a structural gap in the quantitative theory of operator approximation by investigating how subspace approximation schemes interact with local reflexivity. Recent literature establishing the duality of scheme-relative approximation numbers, $a_n(T,Q) = a_n(T^{**}, Q^{\perp\perp})$, has relied on imposing an Extended Local Reflexivity Property (ELRP) as an independent axiom. In this note, we establish a dichotomy. For infinite-dimensional admissible schemes, we prove they naturally generate complete nests, perfectly satisfying the topological hypotheses of the Oja-Veidenberg Nest Principle of Local Reflexivity. Conversely, we demonstrate that for approximation schemes with finite-dimensional components, the bidual geometry collapses. Consequently, the ELRP is entirely superfluous, and the duality of approximation numbers holds unconditionally for all bounded linear operators via an elementary isometric restriction.

</details>
